tamiḻ ilakkaṇap
pērakarāti




poruḷ yāppu - 2



āciriyar
paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpālaiyar





tamiḻmaṇ patikkam



TamilÆ Ilakkan|ap Pe#rakara#ti
(A Tamil Grammatical Encyclopaedia)






Porul@ - Ya#ppu - 2




by T.V. Gopal Iyer
Pandit of the Pondicherry Centre of the École Française d’Extrême-Orient (French School of Asian Studies)









Published by the TamilÆ Man| Pathippakam, Chennai 2005.

Pages: 32+304 = 336
Price: 315/-

muṉṉurai
1979ām āṇṭu navampart tiṅkaḷil tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarāti oṉṟaṉait tokuttu uruvākkum paṇiyil putuc cēriyil uḷḷa tolaik kīḻaip pirañcu ārāyccip paḷḷiyil amarttappaṭṭēṉ. iṭaiyiṭaiyē paṇikkappaṭṭa ēṉaiya paṇi kaḷukku iṭaiyilum akarātip paṇiyait toṭarntu 1995il ōraḷavu ataṉai niṟaivu ceytēṉ. ip paṇiyil eṉakku utavi ceyya amarttappaṭṭa nārāyaṇacāmi aiyar, kumāracāmip piḷḷai, appācāmi mutaliyōr paṇiyiliruntu iṭaiyiṭaiyē viṭuvikkap paṭa ippaṇiyil eṉakku iṟutivarai eṉ iḷaval kaṅkātaraṉē utavum nilai ēṟpaṭṭatu. ippaṇikkut tolkāppiyattiṉ paḻaiya uraikaḷ mutal aṇmaiyil veḷivanta pāvalarēṟu pālacuntaraṉāriṉ teṉmoḻi ilakkaṇam muṭiya uḷḷa pala nūlkaḷum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉavāka vēṅkaṭarāculu reṭṭiyāriṉ eḻuttatikāra ārāycci, ilakkaṇak kaṭṭuraikaḷ, cuppiramaṇiya cāttiriyāriṉ eḻuttatikārac col latikārac kuṟippukkaḷ, pālacuntaraṉāriṉ tolkāppiya ārāycci kāṇṭikaiyurai pōṉṟa cila nūlkaḷē mēṟkōḷkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil veḷivanta ilakkaṇam paṟṟiya kaṭṭuraikaḷ pala iruppiṉum avaiyellām ivvakarāti yil iṭam peṟavillai.
ivvakarāti pala tokutikaḷākap pala talaippup paṟṟip pirittuk kūṟappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, ellā ilakkaṇa vakaikaḷukkum potuvāṉa coṟkaḷ eṅku iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa eṉpataṉai aṟitalil ciṟu cikkal ēṟpaṭum. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka ‘vaḻakku’ eṉṟa col tolkāppiyac ciṟappup pāyirattil mutaṟkaṇ varuvatāl accol pāyiram paṟṟiya pakutiyil viḷakkappaṭṭirukkum. talaippuk kaḷiṉ akarāti amaikkappaṭṭapiṉ attakaiya coṟkaḷiṉ iruppiṭam aṟital eḷitākum.
ivvakarātip paṇiyil iṟuti meypput tiruttutal mutaliya vaṟṟil eṉ iḷaval kaṅkātaraṉē muḻumaiyāka īṭupaṭṭa pōti lum, eṉ tampi tiruttuvataṟku muṉṉarē meyppukkaḷ tiruttattil īṭupaṭṭuc ceyaṟpaṭṭa cāṉṟōr aṉaivaraiyum naṉṟiyoṭu nōkkukiṉṟēṉ. 17 tokutikaḷāka amaiyum inta nūliṉai amaippataṟku eṉakku eṉ tampi valakkaiyāka utavuvatu pōlavē, innūlaip patippikkum iḷavaḻakaṉārukku utaviya patippaka utaviyāḷarkaḷ celvaṉ ce. caravaṇaṉ, celvaṉ i. iṉiyaṉ, celvaṉ mu. kalaiyaracaṉ, araṅka. kumarēcaṉ, vē. taṉacēkaraṉ, 
mu.nā. irāmacuppiramaṇiya irācā, nā. veṅkaṭēcaṉ, ila. tarmarācu ākiyōr innūl cemmaiyāka veḷivarap peritum muyaṉṟuḷḷa ceyalaip pōṟṟukiṟēṉ. ivarkaḷ nōynoṭi iṉṟip pallāṇṭu vāḻa vāḻttukiṟēṉ.
innūlai veḷiyiṭa utaviya eṅkaḷ tolaik kīḻaip pirañcu ārāyccippaḷḷi niṟuvaṉattārukku naṉṟiyait teri vittukkoḷkiṟēṉ.
tokutikaḷ 17 : eḻuttu - 2, col - 4 , poruḷ - 11 (akam - 4, puṟam - 1, aṇi - 2, yāppu - 2, pāṭṭiyal, pāyiram, marapiyal - 1, meyppāṭu, nāṭakam, aḷavai, niyāyam ēṉaiya - 1)

ti.vē. kōpālaiyar


mukavurai
eḻuttatikāra ilakkaṇap pērakarātiyil, tolkāppiyam mutalāka iṉṟu nammiṭaiyē vāḻntu varum ilakkaṇa ilakkiya moḻiyiyal pēraṟiñarām ca.pālacuntaraṉār ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil iyaṟṟiya  ‘teṉṉūl’ muṭiya, iṉṟu namakkuk kiṭṭuvaṉavāka nilavi varum ciṟanta ilakkaṇa nūl varicaiyil iṭampeṟum nūlkaḷum uraikaḷum iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
ikkālattil tolkāppiyak kaṭal eṉṟu pōṟṟappaṭum annūl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa kālattē atu ciṟunūlākavē yākkap peṟṟatu. ataṉ eḻuttup paṭalattil uḷḷa 9 iyalkaḷilum vitik kappaṭuvaṉa ellām karuviyum ceykaiyum eṉa iruvakaippaṭum.  avaṟṟuḷ karuvi nūlmarapu mutaliya nāṉku ōttum, ceykai tokaimarapu mutaliya eñciya aintu ōttum ām.  karuvitāṉum potuvum ciṟappum eṉa iruvakaittu. mutal mūṉṟu ōttum potukkaruvi; ceykai oṉṟaṟkēyuriya puṇariyal ciṟappuk karuvi.
nūlmarapu, nūliṉatu marapu paṟṟiya peyarkaḷākiya eḻuttu - kuṟil - neṭil - uyir - mey - meyyiṉ vakaikaḷ - eḻuttukkaḷiṉ māttirai - iṉṉa meykku iṉṉamey naṭpeḻuttu, pakai yeḻuttu eṉpataṉaik kuṟikkum meymmayakkam - meyyeḻut tukkaḷ, ekara okara uyirkaḷ, kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram eṉṉu mivai puḷḷipeṟutal - makarak kuṟukkam uṭpeṟu puḷḷiyum kōṭal - cuṭṭu - viṉā - aḷapeṭai - eṉpaṉa paṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.
aṭuttu, moḻimarapu kūṟum vitikaḷ nūlmarapiṉ oḻipāka amaintuḷḷaṉa. itaṉkaṇ kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, āytam, aḷapeṭai, moḻiyākkam, īroṟṟuṭaṉilai, makarak kuṟukkam, aikārak kuṟukkam, pōliyeḻuttukkaḷ, moḻi mutalil varum eḻuttukkaḷ, moḻiyīṟṟil varum eḻuttukkaḷ - eṉpaṉa iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa.
piṟappiyal, uyir - mey - cārpeḻuttukkaḷ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ piṟappiṭaṅkaḷum muyaṟciyum paṟṟi moḻikiṟatu.
puṇariyalil, ellāmoḻikaḷiṉ iṟutiyum mutalum mey uyir eṉṟa iraṇṭaṉuḷ aṭaṅkum; peyarum toḻilum eṉṟō toḻilum peyarum eṉṟō perumpāṉmaiyum coṟkaḷ puṇarumiṭattu iyalpākavum tirintum puṇarum; puṇarvaṉa nilaimoḻiyiṉ īṟṟeḻuttum varumoḻiyiṉ mutaleḻuttum ākiya iraṇṭumē; coṟkaḷ vēṟṟumaipporuḷ paṟṟiyum al vaḻipporuḷ paṟṟiyum puṇarum; iṭaiyē cāriyai varappeṟum; cila cāriyaikaḷ uruttirintum puṇarum; eḻuttuccāriyaikaḷ ivai, uṭampaṭumey ivai - eṉa icceytikaḷaik kāṇalām.
tokaimarapu eṉṉum aintām ōttiṉkaṇ, uyirīṟum puḷḷiyīṟum uyirmayaṅkiyaluḷḷum puḷḷi mayaṅkiyaluḷḷum īṟukaḷtōṟum virittu muṭippaṉavaṟṟai orōvoru cūttirat tāl tokuttu muṭipu kūṟappaṭuvaṉavum, uyartiṇaippeyarp puṇarcciyum viravuppeyarp puṇarcciyum iraṇṭām mūṉṟām vēṟṟumaiyurupu ēṟṟa peyarppuṇarcciyum, cila iṭaiccoṟ kaḷatu muṭipum, eṇ niṟai aḷavup peyaruḷ cilavaṟṟatu puṇarcci yum kūṟappaṭṭuḷa.
urupiyal, urupēṟṟa peyar cāriyai peṟṟum peṟāmalum orōvaḻi neṭumutal kuṟukiyum varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumāṟu kūṟukiṟatu. uyirmayaṅkiyalilum puḷḷimayaṅkiyalilum alvaḻip puṇarcci perumpālum eḻuvāyttoṭarkkē koḷḷappaṭu kiṟatu.  vēṟṟumaippuṇarcci vēṟṟumaiyurupukaḷ tokka vēṟ ṟumaip puṇarccikkē koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu.  ippuṇarccikaḷ potuvākak kūṟappaṭṭālum, āciriyar uyartiṇaippeyar - viravuppeyar - kiḷaippeyar - nāṭpeyar - tiṅkaṭ peyar - eṇ niṟai aḷavup peyar - eṉpaṉavaṟṟai vitantōtiyē muṭikkum karuttiṉarātaliṉ, ippuṇarccikaḷ aḵṟiṇaiyil, kiḷai mutalākac collappaṭṭa appeyarkaḷ nīṅkalāṉa ēṉaiyavaṟ ṟiṟkē kōṭal āciriyar karuttām.
āciriyar īreḻuttorumoḻi eṉṟu kūṟiyamai īreḻuttuk kuṟṟukarac collait tam maṉattuk koṇṭamaiyālām.  kuṟṟiya lukaram puḷḷiyīṟu pōla uyirēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukkum. uyiraḷa peṭai eṉpatu neṭilai aṭuttu varum otta iṉakkuṟṟeḻuttē.  puṇarcciyil tolkāppiyaṉār kuṟippiṭum eḻuttuppēṟaḷa peṭai piṟkālattē vaḻakku iṟantatu eṉṟu kōṭal takum.
tolkāppiyattai aṭuttu nāṭṭil ciṟappāka vaḻaṅki vantatākak karutappaṭum avinayam irācapavittirap pallavataraiyaṉ uraiyoṭu 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai vaḻakkiliruntu vanta ceyti naṉṉūl mayilainātar uraiyālēyē peṟappaṭukiṟatu.  annūlum ataṉuraiyum vaḻakkiṟantu viṭṭaṉa.
tolkāppiyattai aṭuttu iṉṟu vaḻakkil irukkum aintilakkaṇa nūl peruntēvaṉār uraiyoṭu kūṭiya vīra cōḻiyamē. pallavar kālattilē “pāratatēyattu vaḻakkilirukkum ellā moḻikaḷukkum tāy camaṟkirutamē” eṉṟa karuttu makka ḷiṭaiyē uruvāka, atu 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭu muṭiya uṟutiyākak koḷḷappaṭṭu vantatu.  11 ām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavil tōṉṟiya vīra cōḻiyam ikkaruttaiyuṭkoṇṭu iyaṟṟappaṭṭa ilakkaṇamē.
vīracōḻiyattiṉkaṇ, uyireḻuttukkaḷai aṭuttu mey yeḻuttukkaḷiṉ muṉṉar neṭuṅkaṇakkil āytam iṭam peṟṟa ceyti kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  makarak kuṟukkam mēlē peṟum puḷḷiyōṭu uḷḷēyum oru puḷḷi peṟum eṉṟa nuṭpamāṉa ceyti innūliṉ uraiyiṉkaṇṇēyē kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.  vaṭamoḻip puṇarcciyil alvaḻi, vēṟṟumai eṉṟa poruḷ paṟṟiya pākupāṭu illai.  ākavē, vīracōḻiyac cantip paṭalattilum alvaḻi vēṟṟumaip pākupāṭu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭilatu.  varumoḻi mutalil uyirvariṉ kuṟṟiyalukaram keṭum eṉṉum ceyti innūlilēyē mutaṟkaṇ kuṟippiṭappaṭa, acceyti nēminātam naṉṉūl mutaliya piṉ ṉūlkaḷ palavaṟṟilum iṭampeṟalāyiṟṟu.  vaṭamoḻiccoṟkaḷ tamiḻolikkēṟpat tirittu vaḻaṅkappaṭumāṟṟiṟku innūl kūṟum vitikaḷē naṉṉūl mutaliya piṉṉūlkaḷilum koḷḷappaṭalāyiṉa.  vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa ‘na’ eṉṟa etirmaṟai muṉoṭṭu varumoḻi yōṭu iṇaiyumiṭattu ēṟpaṭum tiripukaḷai innūl iyampiṭa, ataṉai nēminātamum ēṟṟukkoḷḷa, naṉṉūl ataṉai nekiḻttu viṭṭatu, ḷakarattiṟkuk kūṟum puṇarcciviti ḻakarattiṟkum, in nūlāciriyarkku muṟpaṭṭa ilakkiyaṅkaḷil piṉpaṟṟappaṭavē, in nūlāciriyar ilakkiyam kaṇṭu ataṟku ilakkaṇam varaintuḷḷa ceyti innūlil putitāka iṭam peṟukiṟatu.  icceytiyai nēminātam naṉṉūl pōṉṟa piṉṉūlkaḷ kuṟippiṭavillai.  uṭampaṭumeyyai innūlāciriyar ‘i ī ai vaḻi yavvum, ēṉai uyirvaḻi vavvum, ē muṉ ivvirumaiyum, eṉṟu mutaṉmuṟaiyāka varaiyaṟuttuk kūṟiyavarāvar. “aḷapeṭai mūṉṟu māttirai peṟum; atu neṭilum kuṟilum iṇain tolikkum ōroliyē” eṉṟa ivaratu koḷkaiyē, perumpālum piṉṉūlār palarālum mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭatu. ē yā e - eṉpaṉa col mutalil viṉāvākum eṉṟa ivar karuttaip piṟkālattu nūlār palarum ēṟṟukkoṇṭavarāvar.
aṭuttu vanta nēminātamum, neṭuṅkaṇakku varicaiyai , uyir - aṭuttu āytam - aṭuttu mey - eṉṟē kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. in nūluḷ kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram, aikārak kuṟukkam eṉṉum ivaṟṟōṭu oḷakārak kuṟukkamum kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. “uyiraḷa peṭai neṭiloṭu kūṭiya ōroliyām iṉak kuṟṟeḻuttu; atu mūṉṟu māttirai peṟum” eṉṟu vīracōḻiyattai oṭṭi nēminātam nuvalkiṟatu.  vaṭamoḻit tattitānta nāmaṅkaḷum etir maṟai yuṇarttum nakara muṉṉoṭṭup puṇarcciyum vīracōḻi yattaip piṉpaṟṟiyē kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  vīracōḻiyam vitikkum vaṭamoḻiyākkam nēminātattil illai.
tolkāppiyattai aṭuttu makkaḷ uḷḷattē ciṟappāka iṭampeṟuvatu pavaṇanti muṉivar iyaṟṟiya naṉṉūlē.  muta leḻuttum cārpeḻuttum eṉṟa pākupāṭu - cārpeḻuttup pattu eṉpatu - avai ovvoṉṟum paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ - moḻikku mutalil, iṭaiyil, īṟṟil varum eḻuttukkaḷ - pōliyeḻut tukkaḷ - eṉpaṉa eḻuttiyalil iṭampeṟṟuḷa.  uyiraḷapeṭai neṭṭeḻuttiṉ nīṭṭamākiya mūṉṟu māttirai, kuṟil aṟikuṟi yāka varuvatē eṉṟa ceyti kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu.  pataviyalil, pakupatam pakuti - vikuti  mutaliya uṟuppuk kaḷākap pirittuk kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷatu.  pakupata uṟuppukkaḷ viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pataviyal naṉṉūl kuṟippiṭum orumoḻip puṇarcciyākiya putuc ceytiyē. itaṉ iṟutiyil vaṭamoḻi ākkam vīracōḻiyattaip piṉpaṟṟi varaiyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ‘na’ eṉṟa etirmaṟai muṉṉoṭṭup paṟṟiya ceyti pēcappaṭṭilatu.
tolkāppiyattiṟku iḷampūraṇar varainta uraiyaiyum uṭkoṇṭu iyaṟṟappeṟṟa naṉṉūlil tolkāppiyac ceytikaḷ palavaṟṟoṭum avvuraiyāciriyar kuṟippiṭṭa ceytikaḷum iṭampeṟukiṉṟaṉa. tolkāppiyam kuṟikkum puṇariyal - tokai marapu - uyirmayaṅkiyal - kuṟṟiyalukarap puṇariyal - paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ palavum uyirīṟṟup puṇariyaluḷ aṭakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  alvaḻi yāvaṉa ivaiyeṉa viḷakkappaṭukiṟatu. uṭampaṭumey, kuṟṟukaram uyirvarak keṭutal - pōṉṟavai vīracōḻiyattaip piṉpaṟṟiyaṉavām.  naṉṉūlil kāṇappaṭum māṟṟaṅkaḷ ‘marapu nilai tiriyātu’ amaintaṉa eṉpa.  tolkāppiyattiṉiṉṟu naṉṉūl caṟṟē vēṟupaṭṭuk kūṟumiṭaṅkaḷ poruḷnilai tiriyāmaiyāl ‘marapu nilai tiriyā māṭcimai’ yuṭaiyavāy mutalnūṟku malaivupaṭāmal cellum iyaṟkaiya ātalaic civañāṉamuṉivar tamatu pāyiraviruttiyuḷ viḷaṅkak kūṟumāṟu īṇṭuk karutal takum.
ilakkaṇaviḷakkam, naṉṉūl tolkāppiyattoṭu māṟu paṭṭuk kūṟum oru cila iṭaṅkaḷaic cuṭṭi viḷakkukiṟatu. taṉakku muṟpaṭṭa nūlkaḷil viḷakkappaṭum vaṭamoḻiyākkattai iv vilakkaṇanūl nekiḻttuviṭṭatu.
ilakkaṇakkottu, pirayōka vivēkam eṉpaṉavaṟṟil cila ariya puṇarccivitikaḷ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. a - an - na - ni - ku - vi - puṇarap puṇarppatu vaṭamoḻiyil etirmaṟaiyākum eṉṟa ceyti ilakkaṇakkottil viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. tamiḻc ciṟap peḻuttu aintāṉum tirintu āriyaccol vaṭacollākit tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum ceyti ivviraṇṭu nūlkaḷilum viḷakkappaṭu kiṟatu. icceyti naṉṉūlil iṭam peṟṟilatu.
toṉṉūl viḷakkam naṉṉūlaip peritum piṉpaṟṟiyatu; vaṭamoḻiyākkattilum naṉṉūlaip piṉpaṟṟiyuḷḷatu. muttu vīriyattil tīrkkacanti mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku vitikaḷ taṉittaṉiyē kūṟap paṭṭuḷa. kō + il = kōyil, mā + iru = māyiru - mutaliya vaṟṟiṟkut taṉiyē vitikaḷ vakukkappaṭṭuḷa.  cuvāminātattil kuṟippiṭattakum vicēṭamāka ētum iṉṟu. ataṉ āciriya virutta yāppu nayaṉuṟumāṟu illai.
tolkāppiya eḻuttatikārattu iḷampūraṇar urai - nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urai - ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭu moḻiyiyal vittakarām vēṅkaṭarāculu reṭṭiyār, pi.cā. cuppiramaṇiya cāttiriyār ivarkaḷtam ārāyccik kuṟippukkaḷ - ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṟutiyil veḷivanta pālacuntaraṉārtam tolkāppiyak kāṇṭikaiyurai - civañāṉa muṉivar aracañcaṇmukaṉār varain tuḷḷa viruttiyuraik kuṟippukkaḷ eṉṉumivaiyum, vīracōḻiyam peruntēvaṉār urai - nēminātam vayiramēkaviruttiyurai - naṉṉūl mayilainātar, caṅkaranamaccivāyar,  irāmānucak kavirāyar, caṭakōparāmānucāccāriyār āṟumukanāvalar eṉṟiṉṉōrtam uraikaḷ - eṉṉum ivaiyum ēṉaiya mūla nūlkaḷiṉ ceyti kaḷoṭu tokukkappaṭṭu ivveḻuttatikāra ilakkaṇap pērakarāti uruvākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
itaṉkaṇ kāṇappaṭum kuṟṟam kuṟaikaḷai nallaṟiñar perumakkaḷ uriya kāraṇam cuṭṭik kāṭṭuvārāyiṉ, appiḻai pāṭukaḷ aṭutta patippil kaḷaiyappaṭum. aṉṉa tiruttaṅkaḷ naṉṟi yaṟitalōṭu ēṟkappaṭum.

ti.vē. kōpālaiyar

Eva Wilden
École Française d’Extrême-Orient	16&19 Dumas Street,
Pondicherry centre	Pondicherry.
Aug. 2005
Introduction
Tamil grammar and poetics are old and venerable disciplines interwoven into a complex system the beginnings of which are lost in legend. What is fact, however, is that we are looking back on a textual tradition representing the thought of almost two millennia: a continuous discourse on Tamil language and literature, but also a dispute with other systems of knowledge, most notably the Sanskrit grammatical and poetological traditions. To give a rough chronology, for the first millennium, we have one great treatise encompassing the whole field and developing the basic structure that is taken up, with some modifications and extensions, by the whole later tradition. This is the famous Tolka#ppiyam, consisting of three parts, two of them devoted to two different aspects of grammar, which has been ever since split into two sections, namely ElÈuttu (phonology) and Col (morphology and syntax), while the third part treats of Porul@ (poetics). It is followed by a small work specialising in a particular field of poetics, namely that of Akam (love poetry), called the IrÈaiyan_a#r Akapporul@. 
The second millennium, probably a time of socio-cultural upheaval, sets in with a voluminous commentary tradition not only for the treatises that had been written so far, but also for wide parts of the older literature. In fact our understanding of the meaning of the older texts is basically indebted to these commentaries. Nevertheless, there is a parallel development of new treatises in all sub-disciplines, mirroring the confrontation with the change of language, the arising of new literary forms and the massive impact of North-Indian, i.e. Sanskritic modes of thinking and writing in the Tamilian South. To mention just a few of the most important titles, among the inclusive texts – comprising, just as the Tolka#ppiyam, the whole range of the field – there are the heavily Sanskritised Vi#raco#lÈiyam of the 11th century, and the Tamil-conservative Ilakkan|a Vil@akkam of the 17th century. Both of them extend the original structure of three sections, dealing with ElÈuttu (phonology), Col (morphology and syntax), and Porul@ (poetics), by another two subsumed under Porul@, namely Ya#ppu (metrics) and An|i (figures of speech). Among the influential treatises devoted exclusively to grammar we may list the Nan_n_u#l (12th century), the standard book on Tamil grammar after the Tolka#ppiyam, and the Pirayo#ka Vive#kam (17th century), again very Sanskritic. Poetics, for its part, seems to have been an even more fruitful domain, creating a number of branches with various specia-lisations. The first independent text on metrics is the Ya#pparun)-kalakka#rikai (10th century); the most notable exponent of systematic Akam poetics is the Nampi Akapporul@ (12th century), while the Pur_am genre (heroic poetry) is represented by the Pur_apporul@ven|pa#-ma#lai (9th century).
The encyclopaedia presented here is an attempt to render accessible this wealth of materials to specialists and also to non-specialists. The vast topology and terminology of Tamil grammar and poetics are represented by key terms which are explained with reference to the corresponding su#tras in the treatises and additional explication from the various commentaries. The whole work comprises 17 volumes, structured in the traditional way into the three sections ElÈuttu (phonology), Col (morphology and syntax) and Porul@ (poetics), where 2 volumes fall on ElÈuttu, 4 on Col, and 11 on the various sub-disciplines subsumed under poetics: 2 for Ya#ppu (metrics), 2 for An|i (figures of speech), 4 for Akam (love topics), 1 for Pur_am (heroic topics), 1 for Pa#t@t@iyal (literary genres), Pa#yiram (prefaces) and Marapiyal (word usage), and finally 1 for Meyppa#t@u (physical manifestation), Na#t@akam (drama), Al@avai (valid means of knowledge), A#nantakkur|r|am (collocations to be avoided), Niya#yam (logic) and ValÈuvamaiti (poetic licence). The last of these volumes contains a bibliography.
This sort of work of synthesis has long been a desideratum of research: it gives erudite references to a vast range of technical Tamil texts which are, for the most part, not well understood today. Some of the texts are hard to come by – unless in the editions of the author of this encyclopaedia (on whom more below) –  most of them are not translated into any other language, general introductions into the field are few, and even fewer are written in languages more easily accessible to the general reader (like English or French). There has been more than one project comparable in range in recent years, most prominently the Encyclopaedia of Tamil literature of the Institute of Asian Studies, Chennai (in English language), but sadly this opus has not yet seen more than 3 volumes, the last one already nine years old and reaching only the letter “ai”. All the more reason for scholars interested in Tamil language and literature to be grateful to the author of the present work, the venerable T.V. Gopal Iyer, with his 80 years one of the last living exponents of a great tradition of exegesis.
Space permits here no more than a brief account of the highlights of a long and in many respects exemplary career of a Tamil savant in the 20th century. As well as following a traditional path of education, the worldly marks of which are his two titles Vidvan and Panditam conferred by the University of Madras and the Maturai Tamil Cankam respectively (in 1945 and 1953), Gopal Iyer also acquired the “modern” university degrees of Bachelor of Oriental Language and Bachelor of Oriental Language with Honours at the University of Madras (1951 and 1958). From 1965-1978 he taught in Rajah’s College, Thiruvaiyaru, in which period he already took up his activity of editing works of Classical Tamil, especially theoretical texts. The most important publications from that phase are the Ilakkan|a Vil@akkam in 8 volumes (published in Thanjavur by the Sarasvati Mahal from 1971-1974), the Ilakkan|ak Kottu (Sarasvati Mahal 1973) and the Pirayoka Vivekam (Sarasvati Mahal 1973).
Ever since 1978, Mr. Gopal Iyer’s sphere of activity has shifted to Pondicherry, where he has been (and still is) employed as a research scholar by the École Française d’Extrême-Orient (EFEO) – i.e., the French School of Asian Studies –, a research institution financed by the French government which has 17 research centres spread across Asia, the westernmost of which is that in Pondicherry, and which has the mission of studying Asian (and notably Indian) languages, cultures and religions. In this environment, designed as a meeting place for international research, the enormous preparatory work for this encyclopedia has been accomplished. Part of the voluminous editorial output of Mr. Gopal Iyer during the last 27 years has appeared in a series co-published by the EFEO and the IFP (the French Institute of Pondicherry, another research Institution of the French government in whose premises T.V. Gopal Iyer worked for several years), such as a 3-volume edition of the Te#va#ram, his major contribution to devotional Tamil literature (1984f., 1991), and the Ma#ran_ Akapporul@ (2005). A number of further publications deserve mention, since they concern fundamental texts of the Tamil grammatical and poetological tradition upon which the encyclopaedia is based. Last year he published a 14-volume edition of the complete Tolka#ppiyam with all the commentaries (through Thiru. G. Ela-vazhagan of TamizhMann Pathippagam, Chennai - 17) and this year editions of the Vi#raco#l@iyam and the Ma#r_an_ Alan)ka#ram.
The EFEO is extremely happy that it has been able to contribute its share to this publication of vital importance for the exploration of Tamil literary history, which will be a monument to a most extraordinary man, who has been teacher and adviser, nay, a living encyclopaedia, to so many students of Tamil language, Indian and Western.

A final brief note of thanks to three individuals who were important in bringing this large work to the light of day. The first is Mr. T.V. Gopal Iyer’s younger brother, Mr. T.S. Gangadharan, then of the French Institute of Pondicherry and now of the Pondi-cherry Centre of the EFEO, who wrote the work out in a fair hand.  The second is Dr. Jean-Luc Chevillard, who, years later, had the encyclopeadia digitally photographed when in its yet more voluminous hand-written state and so by his timely intervention prevented the loss of some of its parts.  The third is the publisher, Mr. Ela-vazhagan, of the Thamizh Mann Pathippagam, who had the vision to see the value of this work and took on the task of setting it in type, a labour which took a year and a half and involved five sets of proofs.


īvā vilṭaṉ
pirañcu intiya āyvup paḷḷi				16&19  ṭumās teru
putuccēri maiyam							pāṇṭiccēri,
ākasṭu 2005.
aṟimukavurai
tamiḻ ilakkaṇam miku toṉmai vāyntatu; peru matippiṟ kuriyatu; taṉ kūṟupāṭukaḷ piṇaintu nuṭpamāṉa pēramaippāka uruvākiyuḷḷa ivaṟṟiṉ toṭakkak kālam eḷitil varaiyaṟukka muṭiyāta paḻaṅkālamākum. tamiḻ moḻi paṟṟiyum ilakkiyam paṟṟiyum 2000 āṇṭukaḷāka iṭaiyaṟātu toṭarntu vantuḷḷa cintaṉaikaḷai nām iṉṟu namakkuk kiṭṭiyuḷḷa nūlkaḷiliruntu kāṇkiṟōm. accintaṉaikaḷ piṟamoḻikaḷiṉ (kuṟippāka vaṭa moḻiyiṉ) ilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷōṭu uṟaḻntu vantuḷḷatai yum kāṇkiṟōm. tōrāyamākac coṉṉāl mutal āyiram āṇṭukaḷil tamiḻilakkiyattiṟku muḻumaiyāṉa aṭippaṭaiyāka amaintuḷḷa orē perum pērilakkaṇam tolkāppiyam ākum. (aḵtu avvappoḻutu ciṟcila māṟṟaṅkaḷuṭaṉum virivākkaṅ kaḷuṭaṉum avvāyiram āṇṭukkāla ilakkiyattukkumē aṭip paṭaiyāka amaintatu.) anta orē ilakkaṇamtāṉ pukaḻmiku tolkāppiyam. aḵtu mūṉṟu atikāraṅkaḷ koṇṭatu: eḻuttu (ñāṭinṭiṭaṭipala) col (āṭicayīhṭiṭaṭipala yaṉē ḷulavēyao) poruḷ (ñaṭinavaiuḷa). cila kālam kaḻittup poruḷilakkaṇattiṉ oru pakutiyāṉa akam paṟṟi iṟaiyaṉār akapporuḷ eṉṉum ciṟunūl oṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu.
ki.pi. 1000-kkup piṉṉar tamiḻakattil camutāya - kalācāra māṟṟaṅkaḷ viraivupeṟṟaṉa. akkālakaṭṭattil tōṉṟiyatē virivāka uraiyeḻutum muṟaiyākum. ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷukku maṭṭu maṉṟi, paḻaiya tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil perumpālaṉavaṟṟukkum ivvāṟu uraikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. appaḻanūlkaḷiṉ poruḷait terintukoḷḷap perumaḷavukku avvuraikaḷaiyē nām cārn tuḷḷōm. eṉiṉum akkālakaṭṭattil tamiḻ ilakkaṇattiṉ uṭpirivukaḷ paṟṟip putu nūlkaḷum tōṉṟalāyiṉa. moḻiyil ēṟpaṭṭa māṟṟaṅkaḷ, putiya ceyyuḷ vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam, tamiḻiṉmītu vaṭanāṭṭu aḵtāvatu camaṟkirutam cārnta cintaṉai eḻuttu ākiyavaṟṟiṉ tākkam ivaṟṟaik kāṭṭuvaṉavāka apputu nūlkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. mutaṉmaiyāṉa cilavaṟṟaik kāṇpōm. tol kāppiyam pōl eḻuttu, col, poruḷ mūṉṟaiyum paṟṟi eḻutappaṭṭavai vīracōḻiyamum (mikunta camaṟkirutac cārpuṭaiyatu; 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) ilakkaṇa viḷakkamum (tamiḻ ilakkaṇat tol marapukaḷaik kaṭaippiṭittatu; 17ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) ākum. innūlkaḷ poruḷai yāppu (ānavacaiuḷa) aṇi (kupairacanaḷa ṭika ḷuyīnanauh) eṉṟu mēlum iru pirivukaḷāka ākkiyuḷḷaṉa. eḻuttukkum collukkum maṭṭum ilakkaṇam vakuttaṉavaṟṟuḷ mikutiyum payilappaṭṭavai 12ām nūṟṟāṇṭu naṉṉūlum (tolkāppiyattukkup piṉ tamiḻukku ilakkaṇam eṉṟālē naṉṉūl tāṉ eṉpatē nilaimai) 17 ām nūṟṟāṇṭup pirayōka vivēkamum (mikutiyum camaṟkirutac cārpuṭaiyatu) ākum. poruḷiṉ pirivukaḷ paṟṟiyum uṭ pirivukaḷ paṟṟiyum eḻunta putu ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ mikappala. yāppu paṟṟit taṉiyāka eḻunta mutal ilakkaṇam yāpparuṅkalak kārikai (10ām nūṟṟāṇṭu); akam paṟṟi virivāka eḻutappaṭṭa kuṟippiṭattakka nūl nampi akapporuḷ (12ām nūṟṟāṇṭu); puṟam paṟṟiyatu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālai (9ām nūṟṟāṇṭu).
inta tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarāti mēṟcoṉṉa ilakkaṇac celvaṅkaḷait tamiḻ vallunarkaḷukkum piṟarukkum - oru cērat tokuttut tarum ciṟanta nūl. tamiḻ ilak kaṇattiṉ (poruḷiyal uṭpaṭa) mika virinta parappil kaṇṭa aṉaittu mukkiyamāṉa talaippu ovvoṉṟum akara varicaippaṭi, uriya nūṟpākkaḷum, palvēṟu uraikārarkaḷ kūṟṟukkaḷum tarappaṭṭut teḷivāka viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟatu. eḻuttukku 2, collukku 4, poruḷukku 11 āka 17 tokutikaḷ koṇṭatu iv vakarāti. (poruḷ paṟṟiya 11 tokutikaḷiṉ vakaippāṭu: akam - 4, puṟam - 1, yāppu - 2; aṇi - 2; pāṭṭiyal, pāyiram, marapiyal - 1; meyppāṭu, nāṭakam, aḷavai, āṉantak kuṟṟam, niyāyam, vaḻuvamaiti ākiyavai - 1, eṉpaṉavākum) iṟutit tokutil karuvi nūṟpaṭṭiyalum uḷḷatu.
poruḷkaḷait teḷḷitiṉ uṇarntu muṟaippaṭi viḷakkum ittakaiya pērakarātiyiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāt tēvai neṭunāḷāka āyvulakattāl uṇarappaṭṭuvantatākum. mika virinta iv vilak kaṇanūlkaḷum uraikaḷum tiṭpa nuṭpamāṉa naṭaiyilamaintavai yākaiyāl iṉṟu eḷitil paṭittuṇarattakkavai alla. avaṟṟil kāṇattakum ilakkaṇac celvaṅkaḷuk kellām pulamai cāṉṟa viḷakkaṅkaḷai iṅkuk kāṇalām.
ivvilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ, uraikaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟiṉ accuppaṭikaḷ kūṭa eḷitil kiṭṭuvatillai (kiṭṭiṉum avaiyum ivvakarāti yāciriyar acciṭṭavaiyākavē irukkum; avaraip paṟṟi mēlum cila piṉṉar). avvariya ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷ piṟamoḻikaḷil peyarkkappaṭātavai; ivaṟṟaip paṟṟiya potuvāṉa viḷakka nūlkaḷum cilavē - atuvum āṅkilam, pireñcu pōṉṟa moḻi kaḷil vantuḷḷavai oṉṟiraṇṭēyākum. aṇmaik kālaṅkaḷil ivai pōṉṟa virivāṉa nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭum tiṭṭaṅkaḷ cila vaṟṟuḷ ceṉṉai āciyaviyal niṟuvaṉam āṅkilattil veḷiyiṭat toṭaṅkiya “tamiḻ ilakkiyak kaḷañciyamum” oṉṟu. akkaḷañci yattil mūṉṟu maṭalaṅkaḷē (ai muṭiya) veḷivanta nilaiyil, kaṭanta oṉpatāṇṭukaḷākap paṇi muṭṭuppaṭṭu niṟkiṟatu eṉpatu niṉaikkattakkatu. innilaiyil tamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷilum uraikaḷilum ūṟiya pēraṟiñarkaḷil iṉṟu nammōṭu uḷḷa mikac cilaril oruvarāṉa, 80 vayatu niṟainta vaṇakkattukkuriya 
ti.vē. kōpālaiyariṉ paṭaippāṉa ip pērakarāti tamiḻ ilakkaṇa, ilakkiya aṟiñarkaḷ avarpāl naṉṟi pārāṭṭutaṟkuriya oṉṟākum.
irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭut tamiḻaṟiñarkaḷil palvakaiyilum pōṟṟattakkavaruḷ oruvarāṉa ivvāciriyariṉ neṭiya tamiḻppaṇi kuṟittu mikac ciṟiya aḷavilēyē īṇṭuk kūṟa iyalum. pāram-pariyamāṉa tamiḻp pulamai marapil avar peṟṟa takutikaḷ ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻaka vittuvāṉ (1945); maturait tamiḻc caṅkap paṇṭitam (1953) ākiyavai; attōṭu “navīṉa”p palkalaik kaḻakap paṭṭaṅ kaḷākac ceṉṉaip palkalai kaḻakattil 1951il pi.ō.el paṭṭamum, 1958il pi.ō.el (āṉarcu) paṭṭamum peṟṟuḷḷār. 1965 - 1978il avar tiruvaiyāṟu aracar kallūriyil āciriyappaṇi āṟṟiṉār. appoḻutē patippup paṇiyai, kuṟippāka paḻantamiḻ ilakkaṇa urainūlkaḷaip patippikkum paṇiyait toṭaṅkiṉār. akkāla kaṭṭattil avar patippittavai ilakkaṇa viḷakkam 8 tokutikaḷ (tañcai caracuvati makāl 1971-74), ilakkaṇak kottu (tañcai caracuvati makāl 1973), pirayōka vivēkam (tañcai caracuvati makāl 1973) ākiyavaiyām.
1978iliruntu tiru kōpālaiyar avarkaḷuṭaiya aṟi vāṟṟalaip pireñcu intiya āyvup paḷḷi (viuṭiṭana kucayanḷīyaḷaina ṉa’nuovacaṣīana-ṭīcanaivē ) payaṉpaṭutti varukiṟatu. avar iṉṟu āyvup paṇi ceyyum anniṟuvaṉam pirāṉcu nāṭṭu aracu niti utaviyuṭaṉ naṭaipeṟukiṟatu. av vamaippiṟku āciya nāṭukaḷil mottam 17 āyvumaiyaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. pāṇṭiccēri maiyam uṭpaṭa. ivai āciya (kuṟippāka) intiya moḻikaḷ, paṇpāṭukaḷ, camayaṅkaḷ ākiyavai kuṟittu āyvu ceykiṉṟaṉa. paṉṉāṭṭu āyvāḷarkaḷ oruṅkiṇaintu aṟivup paṇi ceyyum ic cūḻal koṇṭa pāṇṭiccēri maiyattiltāṉ ivvilakkaṇak kaḷañciyam tokukkum māperum paṇi naṭantatu. kaṭanta 27 āṇṭukaḷil tiru kōpālaiyar paṭaitta paṟpala nūlkaḷaiyum pāṇṭiccēriyi luḷḷa pireñcu intiya āyvup paḷḷiyum, pirāṉcu nāṭṭu araciṉ maṟṟoru kīḻaik kalai āyvu niṟuvaṉamāṉa pireñcu niṟuvaṉamum (kucananuh aiḷēvavairavana) veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avvāṟu veḷiyāṉa avar nūlkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavai tamiḻp pakti ilakkiyam cārnta tēvāram (3 tokutikaḷ 1984 mutal 1991 varai), māṟaṉ akap poruḷ (2005) ākiyavai. ippērakarātikku aṭippaṭaiyāka amaintavaiyum kōpālaiyar patippittavaiyumāṉa vēṟu cila nūlkaḷaiyum kuṟippiṭṭākavēṇṭum; avai (tiru. kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ, tamiḻmaṇ patippakam, ceṉṉai - 17 mūlamāka) avar 2004il patippitta tolkāppiyamum (uraikaḷuṭaṉ 14 tokutikaḷ) 2005il avar patippittuḷḷa vīracōḻiyamum māṟaṉ alaṅkāramum ākum.
tamiḻ ilakkiya ilakkaṇa varalāṟṟai āyvu ceytiṭa iṉṟi yamaiyāta karuvi ip pērakarāti. intiya maṟṟum mēlnāṭṭut tamiḻ āyvāḷarkaḷ palarukku ācāṉākavum aṟivuraiyāḷa rākavum, ēṉ naṭamāṭum kalaikkaḷañciyamākavum amainta apūrva māṉa ōr aṟiñariṉ māperuñ cātaṉaiyāka amaivatum itu. eṉavē innūl veḷiyīṭṭil tāṉum paṅku peṟṟu utaviṭa vāyppup peṟṟatu kuṟittup pireñcu intiya āyvup paḷḷi makiḻkiṟatu.

iṟutiyāka immāperum paṇi veṟṟikaramāka naṭaipeṟap perumpaṅku āṟṟiya mūvarukku naṉṟi kūṟiyāka vēṇṭum. mutalā mavar tiru kōpālaiyariṉ iḷaval tiru kaṅkātaraṉ avarkaḷ. avar muṉṉarp pireñcu niṟuvaṉattil paṇi ceytavar. ippoḻutu pi.i.ā.pa. pāṇṭiccēri maiyattil avar āyvaṟiñar, paṇiyil uḷḷār. nūl muḻuvataiyum tam kaippaṭa aḻakāka eḻutiyavar avar. iraṇṭāmavar ṭākṭar ḻāṉ-luk-ceviyar; kaiyeḻuttup pirati muḻuvataiyum ṭijiṭal niḻaṟpaṭamāka eṭuttu eppakutiyum citilamāki aḻintu viṭātapaṭi pārttuk koṇṭavar. mūṉṟāmavar tiru kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ. avar innūliṉ ciṟappaiyum payaṉaiyum uṇarntu atai veḷiyiṭa muṉvantavar. ip perunūlaic cemmai yākak kaṇiṉiyil taṭṭaccu ceytu accukku aṇiyamākkavum, meyppukkaḷai aintu muṟai kavaṉamākat tiruttavum āka oṉṟarai āṇṭukaḷ avarum avaraic cārntavarkaḷum uḻaittuḷḷaṉar.


tamiḻ vāḻka!
talaimāmaṇiyeṉat tamiḻilakkaṇap pērakarātiyai
varaintaruḷiya kalaimāmaṇi,
paṇṭitavittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpālaiyar
avarkaḷai vāḻtti vaḻaṅkum
“pārāṭṭurai”
1	aṉpum aruḷum aṟivum tiruvum
	iṉpamum eḻilum maṉṉiya uruvāyc
	colloṭu poruḷpōl ellum oḷiyum
	pulliya vaṭiveṉa ammai yappaṉāyc
5	centamiḻ mayamāyt tikaḻum cīrcāl
	poṉmalai mēviya puricaṭaik kaṭavuḷ
	nāṉmaṟai navilum nayaṉmiku nāvāl
	cintai cilirkkat teṉṟal ulāvac
	cantaṉam maṇakkum taṇṇiya aruvikūr
10	potiyat tamarntu puviyelām pōṟṟa
	matinalam vaḷarkkum māṇpamai muttamiḻ
	nitivaḷam nalkum nikarilā mātavaṉ
	akattiyaṟ karuḷiya takavamai ilakkaṇa
	mikapperuṅ kalaiyaic cakattavar uṇara
15	palkāp piyanteḷi tolkāp piyaṉmutal
	olkāp pulamai oṇṭamiḻ nūlōr
	iyampiya ilakkaṇap paṉuval yāvaiyum
	uḷanterin turaicey iḷampū raṇarmutal
	cēṉā varaiyar, teyvac cilaiyār,
20	āṉāp pulamaip pērā ciriyar
	uccimēṟ pulavarkoḷ nacciṉārk kiṉiyar
	viccai mikkanak kīra ṉāroṭu
	civañāṉa muṉivar, cuvāmi nātaṉār
	yāppa ruṅkala virutti mutalāya
25	uraivaḻi yāvaiyum puraiyaṟak kaṟṟaṟintu,
	ariyavai yāvaiyum cintaiyiṟ ṟeḷintu
	kaṟṟatai maṟavāp peṟṟiyoṭu tikaḻum
	aruntiṟaṟ pulavaṉ, perumpē rācāṉ,
	virunteṉa vaṭamoḻi payiṉṟaṟi tiṟalōṉ,
30	āṅkilap pulamaicīr tāṅkiṭum ācāṉ,
	caṅka ilakkiyac cālpoṭu iraṭṭaik
	kāppiya nuṇukkamum cintā maṇiyiṉait
	tērntaṟin tuvanturai virikkum cemmal,
	tēṅkamaḻ amiḻteṉa māṇavar cevikoḷap
35	pāṭam payiṟṟum paṇpamai ācāṉ
	tirumuṟai, tivviyap pirapanta aruḷvaḷam
	niṟaimoḻi yāvum neñcam iṉikka
	uruccey tuvakkum oḷirtamiḻp pāvalaṉ,
	purāṇa itikācap pulamaiyum nuṭpam
40	virāviya kampaṉ kavitaiyum piṟapiṟa
	ciṟṟilak kiyakkaṭal tiḷaittanaṟ kalvimāṉ,
	āḷuṭaip piḷḷaiyum aracum nampiyum
	tāḷuṟac cūḻntu talaiyuṟap paṇintu
	teyvat tamiḻāṟ puṉaintatē vāram
45	meyyaṇin tuvakkum aiyā ṟaṉtikaḻ
	kāvirit tāyiṉ karaimicai yoḷicey
	tiruvaiyā ṟataṉiṟ centamiḻt tāyiṉ
	uḷḷam uvappa utitta taṉayaṉ,
	antaṇar kulattil vantanaṟ cāṉṟōṉ
50	kuṇattāl uyarnta kōpā laiyaṉ,
	aṉpum, aṭakkamum, naṇpamai ceyalum,
	iṉcolum, eḷimaiyum, iyalpō ṭamaintoḷir
	pōtakā ciriyaṉ, putiyaṉa puṉaiyum
	āyvaṟi vāḷaṉ, arumpeṟaṟ kaṭṭurai
55	tarumeḻut tāḷaṉ, mūvartē vārac
	colvaḷam icaivaḷam malkiṭum ñāṉam,
	malarntiṭum kaṟpaṉai, vaṇṇaṉai uḷḷam
	kalantiṭum paktik kavinayam yāvaiyum
	ulakuṇarn tuyya uraivirit tiyampiya
60	palakalai yuṇarnta paṇṭitaṉ ilakkaṇak
	kaṭalpaṭin tellai nilaikaṇ ṭeḻunta
	ācāṉ, taṉpē ruḻaippiṉāl ilakkaṇak
	kalaiccol yāvaiyum kaviṉuṟat tokuttup
	poruḷnilai viḷaṅka akara niralpaṭa
65	ilakkaṇap pēraka rātiyai varaintu
	pērā ciriyar,āy vāḷar, māṇavar
	yāvarum payaṉpeṟak kāviyam pōlat
	tamiḻttāyk kaṇiyāt takavuṟa vaḻaṅkum
	paṇṭita ti.vē. kōpā laiyar
70	vaṇṭamiḻ pōla vaḷamelām mēvi
	maṇṭalam pukaḻap pallāṇṭu pallāṇṭu
	vāḻkeṉap pōṟṟi vāḻttutum uvanteṉ.

tañcāvūr		
22.08.2005					 pāvalarēṟu ca. pālacuntaraṉār


“kaṉi tantāl kaṉi
uṇṇavum vallirō”
mutuperum pulavar ilakkaṇak kaṭal uyartiruvāḷar 
ti.vē. kōpālaṉār, tamiḻvaḷak koṭaiyāka, arumpatippup peruntakai kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār vaḻaṅkum, ‘tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarāti’ maṭalaṅkaḷ patiṉēḻaṉaiyum oruṅkē kaṇṭatum,
“kārē! nērē tāṉ,
vāriyuṇṭu; vārimoṇṭu vāriyuṇṭu, vāṉiruṇṭu
pēri koṇṭu nītiraṇṭu pey”
eṉṉum, vāṉmaḻaip pāṭṭiṉ ‘tēṉpiḻi’veṉa eṉakku avai iṉpa mūṭṭiṉa.
kōpālaṉār mūḷaik kūrppum,
iḷavaḻakaṉār patippu īrppum,
oruṅkē vayappaṭuttiya iṉpattil,
‘aṉṉai vāḻka’, ‘ayarāt toṇṭarkaḷum vāḻka’
eṉa eṉṉuḷ vāḻttiṉēṉ.
kōpālaṉār niṉaiviṉ ēntal; ilakkaṇamā ilakkiyamā, nūlōṭu uraiyum neñcak kaḷaṉil varappaṭutti vaippāka vaittuk kākkum karuvūla vāḻvar.
kaṟṟatu oṉṟaiyum kaiviṭāk ‘karumi’yeṉak kavarntu koṇṭa tamiḻvaḷattai, eṉṟum eṅkum evarkkum ‘tarumi’ eṉa vāri vaḻaṅkum vaḷḷiyar. avartam ivvakarātik koṭai, tamiḻulaku kālattāl peṟṟa kaviṉ paricilām!
eṉṉai yaṟiyātē eṉakkoru perumitam; ēkkaḻuttam; “ittaku pāriya ilakkaṇat tokutikaḷai immoḻi oṉṟai yaṉṟi, emmoḻitāṉ peṟakkūṭum?” eṉṉum eṇṇattiṉ viḷaivē aḵtām!
ammamma!
eḻuttu	-	iraṇṭu maṭalaṅkaḷ
col	-	nāṉku maṭalaṅkaḷ
iv āṟu maṭalaṅkaḷait tāṉē maṟṟai maṟṟai moḻikaḷ peṟakkūṭum! poruḷilakkaṇam eṉpatoṉṟu koḷḷā moḻikaḷ, eppaṭit tamiḻaip pōl poruḷ ilakkaṇa maṭalaṅkaḷaip peṟa vāykkum?
poruḷilakkaṇa maṭalaṅkaḷ, eḻuttu, col maṭalaṅ kaḷaip pōṉṟa eṇṇikkaiyiṉavō?
akapporuḷ		-	nāṉku maṭalaṅkaḷ.
puṟapporuḷ		-	oru maṭalam
yāppu			-	iraṇṭu maṭalaṅkaḷ
aṇi			-	iraṇṭu maṭalaṅkaḷ
meyppāṭu mutalaṉa	-	oru maṭalam
pāṭṭiyal mutalaṉa	-	oru maṭalam
ākap patiṉoru maṭalaṅkaḷ. mottamākak kūṭutal 17 maṭalaṅkaḷ.
moḻi ārvalarkkup ‘perumitam’ uṇṭākumā? uṇṭākātā?
ipperumitattūṭēyē ōr ‘ēkkam’:
ariya āyvuk kuṟippukaḷ vaḻaṅkit teḷivuṟutta valla āciriyar, avaṟṟai aritāka mēṟkoṇṭatai aṉṟip peritāka allatu muṟṟāka mēṟkoṇṭilarē eṉpatē avvēkkam.
e-ṭu:
‘akara mutal ṉakara iṟuvāy’ eṉpaṉa iḷampūraṇarukku aṉmoḻit tokai; nacciṉārkkiṉiyarukku eḻuvāyt toṭarkaḷ - eṉṟu kāṭṭum tokaiyāciriyar,
“akarattai mutalākavum ṉakarattai iṟutiyākavum uṭaiya eṉṟu poruḷ ceyyiṉ akaramutal - ṉakara iṟuvāy eṉpaṉa iraṇṭām vēṟṟumait tokai” eṉṟu tam teḷivai iyaikkiṟār (eḻuttu 1:22)
naṉṉūlār aḷapeṭaiyaic cārpeḻuttāka eṇṇiṉār. āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār aḷapeṭaiyai uyireḻuttuḷ aṭakkik koṇṭār eṉpataic civañāṉa muṉivar cūttira virutti koṇṭu teḷivikkiṟār tokaiyāciriyar. (eḻuttu 1:44)
“makarak kuṟukkam mēlāl peṟum puḷḷiyoṭu uḷḷum puḷḷi peṟum eṉṟa uraiyāciriyar karuttu mikat teḷḷitu. ‘uṭpeṟu puḷḷi uruvākummē’ (to.e. 14) eṉṟa nūṟpāviṟku uṇmaiyurai kāṇa utavukiṟatu” eṉṟu teḷivikkiṟār tokai yāciriyar. (eḻuttu 2: 263)
‘vēṇṭā kūṟi vēṇṭiyatu muṭittal’, eṉṉum talaippil, “tēvaiyaṟṟatu pōṉṟa oru ceytiyaik kūṟi ataṉāl, nūṟpākkaḷil kūṟappaṭāta maṟṟōr iṉṟiyamaiyāta ceytiyaip peṟappaṭa vaittal” eṉṟu viḷakkum tokaiyāciriyar, nuṇ māṇ nuḻaipulam kaṟpavar teḷivukku nalvaḻi kāṭṭukiṉṟatu. (eḻuttu 2:265)
āṉāl, ittakaiyavai peritum iṭam peṟāmal ‘tokai yaḷavoṭu’ niṉṟu viṭukiṉṟatu eṉpatē avvēkkam. tokai yāvatu, tokuppu.
‘kuṟṟiyalukaram oṟṟu īṟē’ eṉpatōr āyvu eṉṟāl,
‘kuṟṟiyalukaram uyir īṟē’ eṉpatōr āyvu ātal kāṭṭappaṭavēṇṭum allavō! etu cevvitu eṉpatait teḷivittal iṉṟēṉum, teḷivikkak karuvitantatu ākum aṉṟō! ivvāṟu, piṉṉāyvāḷar eṇṇaṟṟōr āyvukaḷ koḷḷappaṭāmai maṭṭumaṉṟu; taḷḷappaṭutal mikat teḷi vākiṉṟatu.
tokaiyāciriyarāl cuṭṭappaṭum aracañcaṇmukaṉār (pāṭṭiyal 15) āyvu ettakaiyatu eṉiṉ, ‘naṭunilai piṟaḻāmal ārāyntu eḻutuvatil vallunar’ eṉat tokaiyāciriyarālēyē pārāṭṭappaṭum takaiyatām. accaṇmukaṉār, ‘piramāṇam ākāta nūlkaḷ’ eṉpataiyum pativu ceykiṟār tokaiyāciriyar. atu,
“ciṉṉūl(nēminātam), naṉṉūl, vīracōḻiyam, ilakkaṇak kottu, pirayōka vivēkam, cūttiravirutti, ilakkaṇa viḷakkac cūṟāvaḷi, ilakkaṇa viḷakkam mutalākap pala, tolkāppiyattiṉ vaḻippaṭac ceyyappaṭiṉum āciriyaṉatu karuttuṇarāmal marapu nilai tiriyac ceyyappaṭṭamaiyāṉ piramāṇamākāta nūlkaḷ ām. (piramāṇam nūlneṟikkuc cāṉṟāka - eṭuttuk kāṭṭākat tikaḻum vāymaiyākiya takuti; niyāya aḷavaikaḷāl uṟutippāṭu) ivvāṟu kuṟippar aracañcaṇmukaṉār (pā.vi. pak. 104-105)” eṉkiṟār (pāṭ. 142) eṉpatu.
tokaiyāciriyar, ic cāṉṟaip poṉṉē pōlap pōṟṟiyiruppiṉ, ataṉ peruñciṟappu eppaṭi iruntirukkum? taṉ peyarukku ēṟpat ‘tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarāti’ eṉpataṟkut tāṉē taṉṉikarilāt talaimai koṇṭirukkum!
ippiramāṇamākā nūlkaḷait taḷḷiyiruppiṉ ippāriya nilai irukkumō eṉiṉ, avveṇṇam piḻaipaṭṭa eṇṇa mām!
‘toṭarnilaic ceyyuḷvakaip peyar’ eṉṉum talaippil kūṟappaṭum aḷavukōl tāṉā iṉṟum uḷḷatu? caturakarātiyum pirapanta marapiyalum kūṟuvaṉa 96 maṭṭumē. maṟṟai nūlkaḷāl aṟivaṉa ataṟkum uṭpaṭṭaṉavē. āṉāl, ciṟṟilakkiya virivākkap parappu ettaṉai? 381 vakaiyeṉpatai em, ‘ilakkiya vakaimai akarāti’yil kāṇalām. avaṟṟiṉ mēlum itukāl virin tuḷḷaṉa. poruḷatikāra āyvō, veḷḷap perukkāki uḷḷatu. kālṭuvelār, ñāṉap pirakāca aṭikaḷār, pāvāṇar aṉṉavarkaḷ āyvu tamiḻilakkaṇa āyvukaḷ alla eṉat taḷḷap paṭṭuviṭāvē! avaṟṟai nōkkiṉāl, vēṇṭāc cērppukaḷai vilakki, vēṇṭum cērppukaḷai iṇaittuk koṇṭāl iṉṉum patiṉ maṭalaṅkaḷ mikum eṉpatai, niṉaiviṉ ēntal - cōrvaṟiyāc cuṭar - kōpālaṉār koḷvārē eṉiṉ, ittamiḻ ilakkaṇappērakarāti ottatōr akarāti iṉṟām eṉa malaimēl ēṟi muḻakkamiṭalām allavō!
ittokaiyāciriyap perumakaṉārai allār oruvar, ipperuṅ kaṭappāṭṭai mēṟkoṇṭu ittaku paṇi ceytal aritu! avartam muḻutuṟu oppaṭaippiṉ pēṟu atu. aṉṟiyum, tammaip pōlavē tam uṭaṉpiṟappukaḷaiyum aḻuntip payilavum ārvak kaṭaṉāṟṟavum payiṟṟi irukkum payiṟṟutaṟpēṟu; tamiḻ vāḻvākiya avarait tāṅkutalē tam piṟavip pēṟu eṉakkoṇṭu nayattaku tuṇaiyāyum kuṭumpamākiyum niṟpār keḻutakaip pēṟu; iṉṉavellām, it tamiḻp peruṅkoṭaikku ūṟṟuk kaṇkaḷ aṉṉavām.
iṅkuc cuṭṭappaṭṭavai, tamiḻk kātalāl, tamiḻar paṇ pāṭṭuk kātalāl cuṭṭappaṭṭavai eṉpataik kaṟpār uṇariṉ, ittokutikaḷait tattam kuṭimai vaippākak koḷḷak kaṭamaippaṭṭavarām. āyvukku ippaṭiyoru karuvi eḷitil vāykkumā? āyvukku ellai uṇṭā? ‘aṟitō ṟaṟiyāmai’ kāṇum āyvukku, “maṉitarkāḷ iṅkēvam; kaṉitantāl kaṉi uṇṇavum vallirō” eṉṟu apparaṭikaḷ tamiḻkkōyil vāyil muṉ niṉṟu aḻaittu vaḻikāṭṭuvatu pōla, ayarāt toṇ ṭarkaḷ ti.vē. kōpālaṉārum, kō. iḷavaḻakaṉārum it tokaiyaik kaikōttu niṉṟu kaṉivoṭu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar! nām pēṟeṉap peṟṟup payaṉ koḷvōmāka!

tamiḻttoṇṭaṉ,
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ


‘kuṟuntaṭṭāka’ viḷaṅkupavar
paṇṭitavittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpālaiyar
putuvaikku varukiṉṟavarkaḷ iṅkē iraṇṭu kaṭalkaḷaip pārkkalām. oṉṟu uvarkkaṭal; maṟṟoṉṟu ti.vē. kōpālaiyar eṉṉum nūṟkaṭal. ‘takaṭupaṭu pacumpoṟ cikaraṅkaḷiṉ mukaṭu toṭuttup poṉkoḻittu, maṇivaraṉṟi, māṇikkattoṭu vayiram unti vantu, cala calaveṉa iḻitarum aṇikiḷar aruvi’ pōṉṟa ivaratu poḻiviṉai oru muṟai kēṭkum evarum viyantu, ivar oru nūṟkaṭaltām’ eṉpatai eḷitiṉil ēṟṟukkoḷvar.
‘aḷakkalākā aḷavum poruḷum tuḷakkalākā nilaiyum tōṟṟamum’ koṇṭa malaiyaṉaiya māṇpiṉ aṟiviṉarākiya ti.vē. kōpālaiyar kaṟṟōrtamakku varampākiya takaimaiyar. taṇṭamiḻiṉ mēlāntaramāṉa ilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷaiyum, avaṟṟukkup paṇṭaiyōr uraitta taṇṭamiḻ uraikaḷaiyum iḷamaiyilēyē pativu ceytukoṇṭa kuṟuntaṭṭāka viḷaṅku pavar ip peruntakai. ekkāraṇattālāvatu innūlkaḷil oṉṟai iḻakka nēriṉ kavalaippaṭa vēṇṭiyatillai; ivartam uḷḷap pativiliruntu ataṉai mīṇṭum uruvākkik koḷḷalām.
‘centamiḻt teṉputuvai eṉṉum tirunakarkkup’ pukaḻ cērtta pāratiyār, pāvēntar mutaliya pukaḻmaṇikaḷiṉ varicaiyil iṉṟu kōpālaiyar viṇṇuyar tōṟṟattuṭaṉ viḷaṅkukiṟār. iḷampūraṇar, cēṉāvaraiyar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, pērāciriyar, teyvaccilaiyār, kallāṭar, parimēlaḻakar, civañāṉa muṉivar, kārirattiṉak kavirāyar mutaliya pulavar marapiṉōr pukaḻai yellām tam pukaḻ ākkikkoṇṭa ippulavar peruntakaiyaip putuvaip pulavarulakam pōṟṟi ‘nūṟkaṭal’ eṉṟa ciṟappup paṭṭamaḷittup poṉpōṟ potintu koṇṭatu.
irupattāṟāṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka ivar putuvaip pirañcuk kalai niṟuvaṉattil (viuṭiṭana kucayanḷīyaḷaina) tamiḻāyvup piriviṉ talaimaip poṟuppiṉai mēṟkoṇṭu ariya nūlkaḷ palavaṟṟai āyvuc cempatippukaḷāka veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṟār.
pāratiyāriṉ talaiciṟanta paṭaippukaḷukku vāyppāka irunta putuvaimaṇ, kōpālaiyariṉ pukaḻai eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavac ceyyum uyar patippukaḷ pala uruvāvataṟkum vāytta iṭamāka ilaṅkukiṟatu.
kaṭanta paṉṉīrāṇṭukaḷākap putuvaiyiliruntu veḷivantu koṇṭirukkum ‘teḷitamiḻ’ eṉṉum tamiḻ vaḷarccit tiṅkaḷi taḻil, ivartam paṭaippiṉai ēnti vārāta itaḻē illai. atil ‘ilai maṟai kaṉikaḷ’ eṉṉum talaippil, tamiḻilakkaṇa ilakkiya nūlkaḷilum uraikaḷilum ilai maṟai kaṉikaḷāka maṟain tirukkum ariya ceytikaḷait tiraṭṭik kaṭṭuraikaḷākat tantu varukiṟār.
alāṉ taṉiyēlu (haṭayaṉē ṉuyannaiṭaṭira) eṉṉum mēṉāṭṭaṟiñariṉ maṇimēkalai āṅkila moḻipeyarppup paṇikkut tuṇai ceytatum, cēṉāvaraiyattiṉ pirañcu moḻiyākkattiṟkut tuṇai niṉṟatum ivartam āṅkila aṟivukkuc cāṉṟu pakarum.
‘tolkāppiyap paḻaiya uraikaḷiṉ cempatippu’, ‘kal veṭṭukkaḷil nāyaṉmārkaḷ paṟṟiya aruñ ceytikaḷ’ ākiyaṉa iṉi veḷivara irukkum ivartam nūlkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkavai.
ivartam paṇikaḷil mikamikap payaṉ viḷaikkum ariya periya paṇi inta ‘tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarāti’. tamiḻilakkaṇam kaṟpōrukkum, ilakkaṇa āyvāḷarkaḷukkum kai viḷakkākap payaṉpaṭakkūṭiya iv vakaravaricai irupattaintu āṇṭuk kāla pēruḻaippāl eḻuti muṭikkappeṟṟatu. eppōtu veḷivarumō eṉṟu tamiḻaṟiñar ulakam etirpārttiruntatu.
maṟṟavar ariyateṉṟu karutum nalla patippup paṇikaḷai eḷiyateṉṟu ēṟṟu, maṭitaṟṟut tāmē muṉvantu ceyyum tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimaiyāḷar, ‘tamiḻmoḻik kāvalar’ 
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ itaṉai aḻakuṟap patippittu vaḻaṅkukiṟār. ivvariya ceyalāl, iṉpattamiḻ irukkumaḷavum iḷavaḻakaṉ pukaḻum irukkum eṉpatu uṟuti.
	aṉpaṉ,
	irā. tirumurukaṉ.


‘īrattamiḻil āḻaṅkāl paṭṭavar’
paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpālaiyar
ulakap potumaṟaiyai aruḷiya tiruvaḷḷuvap perun takaiyār periyār yār eṉpataṟku ōr ilakkaṇam vakukkiṉṟār. maṉitamēmpāṭṭukkuriyatāṉa, ceyvataṟku ariyatāṉa ceyalai yār purikiṉṟārkaḷō avarkaḷē periyavar eṉkiṉṟār.
kālaṅkaḷtōṟum palvēṟu tuṟaikaḷil maṉitamēmpāṭṭuk kākap palar ceyaṟkariya ceyalkaḷaic ceytuḷḷārkaḷ. anta varicaiyil tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarāti eṉṉum innūlai ti.vē.kōpālaiyarum, inta nūlai veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimaiyāḷar iḷavaḻakaṉārum aṭaṅkuvar.
tamiḻ ilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ orucamayam illāmaṟ pōy viṭṭālum kōpālaiyar oruvar iruntālē pōtum, avaṟṟai mīṭṭuk koṇṭuvantuviṭalām. anta aḷaviṟkut tamiḻ ilakkiya ilakkaṇaṅkaḷait tam niṉaivil vaittiruppavar.
oru tēṉkūṭṭil pala malarkaḷiṉ tēṉkaḷ kalantiruppatu pōl intap pērakarātiyil pala tamiḻilakkaṇa nūlāciriyarkaḷiṉ varaiyaṟaikaḷum pala uraiyāciriyarkaḷiṉ urai vaḷaṅkaḷum kalantuḷḷaṉa.
aṟiñar tilakam kōpālaiyar eppaṭi eḷimaiyāṉava rākavum, āḻamāṉa pulamai uṭaiyavarākavum, paḻaka iṉiyavarākavum irukkiṟārō, appaṭiyē ‘naviltoṟum nūlnayam pōlum payiltoṟum paṇpuṭaiyāḷar toṭarpu’ eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷiṉ karuttukkēṟpa innūlum nammiṭam paḻakukiṉṟatu.
inta nūl pērakarātiyāka uḷḷatāl ilakkaṇam kaṇṭu añcum māṇavarkaḷum tamiḻ ārvalarkaḷum potumakkaḷum akarātip poruḷai aṟivatu pōl eḷitākat tamiḻ ilakkaṇattai aṟintu koḷḷa muṭiyumāṟu uḷḷatu.
innūl tolkāppiyar kālattiliruntu inta nāḷvarai uḷḷa tamiḻ ulakiṟkuk kiṭaitta putumaiyāṉa mutaṉmaiyāṉa muḻumaiyāṉa nūlākum.
palar muyaṉṟu ceyya vēṇṭiya paṇiyai ti.vē.kōpā laiyarē ceytu muṭittuḷḷār. oru palkalaikaḻakamō oru periya āyvu niṟuvaṉamō ceyya vēṇṭiya paṇiyait tamiḻmaṇ patippakam ārvattāl eḷitākac ceytuḷḷatu. tamiḻarkaḷiṉ tavappayaṉē ippaṭiyāyt tamiḻ maṇṇil mukiḻttuḷḷatu.
tamiḻakattil maṭṭumallāmal tamiḻ vaḻaṅkum aṉaittu iṭaṅkaḷilum tirukkuṟaḷukku aṭuttapaṭiyāka nūlakaṅkaḷil iṭampeṟa vēṇṭiya tamiḻnūl innūl eṉpatil aiyam illai.
inta nūlai veḷiyiṭṭa tamiḻmaṇ patippaka iḷavaḻaka ṉārkku namatu veṟṟi vāḻttukkaḷ. inta nūlai aḷittaruḷiya aṟiñartilakam nammuṭaiya ilakkaṇa māmaṇi kōpālaiyarukkut talaiyallāl kaimmāṟillai  eṉṉum paṭiyāṉa tamiḻ vaṇakkaṅkaḷ. vāḻka tamiḻ. vaḷarka tamiḻppaṇpāṭu. velka maṉitanēyam.

aṭiyēṉ.
muṉaivar ‘vaiṇavam’ pārttacārati


‘māntak kaṇiṉi’
paṇṭitavittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpālaiyar
nām caṅkap pulavarkaḷaip pārttilōm! iṭaik kālap pulavarkaḷaiyum urai vallunarkaḷaiyum pārttilōm! āṉāl avarkaḷai yellām nam kālattil pārttiṭa virumpuvōmāyiṉ avarkaḷiṉ uruvāka vāḻntukoṇṭirukkum nūṟkaṭal ti. vē. kōpālaiyar avarkaḷaik kāṇalē cālum.
cāti, mata, vayatu vēṟupāṭiṉṟit tammai aṇukum yārē yāyiṉum ayarvuṟātu māṟṟam koḷḷātu avartam aiyaṅkaṭkut teḷivēṟpaṭuttalum viṉākkaṭku viṭaiyaḷittalumāṉa cīriya tamiḻppaṇiyait toṭarntu āṟṟivarukiṟār.
enta nūlil, entap pakkattil, entap pākiyil, enta variyil uḷḷatu eṉat teḷinta uṇarvuṭaṉ entac collaiyum karuttaiyum cuṭṭik kāṭṭum viyakkattakka māntak kaṇiṉiyāka viḷaṅkukiṟār.
caṅkap pulavarum iṭaik kālap pulavarum urai vallunarum kaiyāṇṭa moḻi mutaleḻuttu, moḻiyiṟuti eḻuttu, puṇarcci neṟikaḷ, piṟa moḻic coṟkaḷaiyum eḻuttukkaḷaiyum nīkkal ākiya tamiḻ ilakkaṇa marapukaḷaip poṉṉē pōl pōṟṟip payaṉpaṭutti varum pāṅku eṇṇi eṇṇi makiḻattakkatākum.
apperumakaṉār vāḻuṅkālattil vāḻtalum, avariṭattu uraiyāṭi makiḻtalum, ēṟpaṭum aiyaṅkaṭku avariṉ collariya viḷakkaṅkaḷaik kēṭṭut teḷitalumākiya ariya pēṟṟiṉai nāṉ eṉatu vāḻnāḷil peṟṟuḷḷēṉ.
avariṉ paranta ilakkiya nūlaṟivum teḷinta ilakkaṇa aṟivum tamiḻarkkum ulakukkum mēlum payaṉpaṭutal vēṇṭum.
avar mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷa eḷiya vāḻvu, cama nōkku, iṉiya pēccu, ellārkkum payaṉpaṭal vēṇṭum eṉṉum perumpaṇpu ākiyavai kaṟṟārai īrkkum taṉmaiyaṉa.
nūṟkaṭalār mēlum pallāṇṭukaḷ vāḻntu tamiḻmoḻi vaḷarccikkup peruntoṇṭāṟṟiṭal vēṇṭum eṉa eṉatu uḷḷam niṟainta viḻaiviṉait terivittukkoḷkiṟēṉ.
aṉpaṉ
iṟaiviḻiyaṉ


patippurai
tamiḻar vāḻviyal ilakkaṇattai varaiyaṟuttuk kūṟum olkāppukaḻ tolkāppiyattiṟkup paḻantamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷ paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpu eḻutiya aṉaittu uraikaḷaiyum tokuttu tolkāppiya nūl patippil ituvarai evarum kaṇṭirāta vakaiyil orēvīccil em patippakam veḷiyiṭṭatai tamiḻulakam naṉku aṟiyum.
tolkāppiya nūlpatippup paṇikku allum pakalum tuṇai yiruntu uḻaittavar paṇṭitavittuvāṉ ti.vē.kōpālaiyar āvār. innūl patippukaḷukku ivarē patippāciriyarāka iruntu em tamiḻp paṇikkup perumai cērttavar. kūraṟivum, perum pulamaiyum, niṉaivāṟṟalum mikka ipperuntakai irupattaintu āṇṭukaḷukku mēlāka tam pēruḻaippāl tokutta tamiḻ ilak kaṇattiṟkāṉa col mūlaṅkaḷai ‘tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarāti’ eṉum perumpaṭaippāka 17 tokutikaḷai uḷḷaṭakki ‘tamiḻ ilakkaṇa’k koṭaiyāka tamiḻ ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkiyuḷḷār.
tamiḻ ilakkaṇa nūl patippu varalāṟṟil tamiḻ ilakkaṇat tiṟkeṉa orē nērattil eḻuttu, col, poruḷ (akam, puṟam, aṇi, yāppu, pāṭṭiyal, pāyiram, marapiyal, meyppāṭu, nāṭakam, aḷavai niyāyam) eṉum varicaiyil pērakarāti veḷivaruvatu ituvē mutal muṟaiyākum.
akarāti eṉpatu oru collukku viḷakkam taruvatu. pērakarāti eṉpatu oru collukku viḷakkamum, mēṟkōḷum cuṭṭuvatu. kaḷañciyam eṉpatu oru collukkup palporuḷ viḷakkam kāṭṭuvatu.
uṅkaḷ kaikaḷil tavaḻum it tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarātit tokutikaḷ tamiḻukkuk karuvūlamāy amaivatākum. tamiḻilakkaṇap perum parappai virivu ceyyum ip peṭṭakattai veḷiyiṭuvatil perumaip paṭukiṟōm. tamiḻ āyvai mēṟkoḷvārkku vairac curaṅkamākavum, tamiḻ ārvalarkaḷukku vaḻikāṭṭum oḷi viḷak kākavum innūl tokutikaḷ varukiṟatu.
toṉmaiyum, muṉmaiyum, tāymaiyum, tūymaiyum, meṉmaiyum, mēṉmaiyum, paḻamaiyum, putumaiyum, iḷamaiyum, mutumaiyum mikkamoḻi nam tamiḻmoḻi. tiraviṭamoḻikaḷukkut tāymoḻi nam tamiḻmoḻi. intiyap perumoḻikaḷukku mūlamoḻi nam tamiḻmoḻi. ulaka moḻikaḷukku mūtta moḻi nam tamiḻmoḻi. intiyap pēraracāl cemmoḻi eṉa aṟivikkap paṭṭuḷḷa in nērattil, ‘tamiḻ ilakkaṇap pērakarāti’yai veḷiyiṭuvatil peru makiḻcci aṭaikiṟōm.
tamiḻ ilakkaṇac curaṅkattait tantuḷḷa paṇṭita vittuvāṉ ti.vē. kōpālaiyaruṭaṉ uṭaṉiruntu  uḻaittavar avar iḷaval ti.vē. kaṅkātaraṉ āvār. ip poṟkuviyal polivōṭu veḷi varuvataṟku tam muḻup poḻutaiyum celaviṭṭavar ivar. iraveṉṟum, pakaleṉṟum pārātu uḻaitta ip perumakka ḷukkum, pērakarātiyiṉ arumai perumaikaḷai matippīṭu aḷittu perumai cērtta putuccēri pireñcu intiya āyvu niṟuvaṉat tukkum, tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkkum em naṉṟi.

ivarkaḷukku em naṉṟi .



yāppu  2



taṉikkuṟil nēracaiyākum iṭaṅkaḷ-
taṉakkuṟilāṉatu taṟcuṭṭu, ēval, kuṟippu, cuṭṭu, viṉā eṉṟa aintum tavira ēṉaiya iṭaṅkaḷil nēracaiyāka vārātu.
e-ṭu : ‘a,ā, iḻantāṉeṉ ṟeṇṇap paṭum’ (nālaṭi. 9)
a ā - antō eṉṉum poruḷatu. īṇṭuk kuṟippiṉkaṇ taṉikkuṟil moḻi mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟu viṭṭicaittu nēracai yāyiṟṟu. ākavē, a ā- nēr nēr eṉka.
e-ṭu :	‘noalaiyal niṉāṭṭai nī’
no eṉa, ēvaṟkaṇ taṉikkuṟil moḻimutaṟkaṇ viṭṭicaittu nēracaiyāyiṟṟu. noalaiyal- nēr nirainēr (no - nōva - varunta; alaiyal, niṉ āṭṭai- niṉatu āṭṭiṉait tuṉpuṟuttātē.)
e-ṭu : 	‘a u  aṟiyā aṟivil iṭaimakaṉē’
au - eḻuttukkaḷaic cuṭṭiyamaiyiṉ taṟcuṭṭāy iraṇṭu kuṟṟuyirkaḷum viṭṭicaittu nērnēr āyiṉa.
e-ṭu : 	‘a avaṉum i ivaṉum u uvaṉum kūṭiyakkāl
e evaṉai vellār ikal’
a avaṉ, i ivaṉ, u uvaṉ - cuṭṭu
e evaṉ - viṉā.
a avaṉum mutaliya nāṉkum, cuṭṭu viṉā iṭaiccoṟ kaḷākiya kuṟṟuyireḻuttukkaḷ viṭṭicaittaliṉ nērnirai nēr ām.
ivai viṭṭicaippiṉallatu maṟṟoṉṟaṉōṭu iyaintu iṉiyavāy naṭappiṉ mutaṟkaṇ nēracai ākā..
‘yarala vaḻaḷa iṭaiyiṉamām ēṉai’
‘tu maruntu tūyaṉavē koṇṭu’
‘yarala vaḻaḷa’ eṉpaṉa taṟcuṭṭiṉkaṇṇum viṭṭicaiyāmai yiṉ yarala - nirainēr; vaḻaḷa - nirainēr eṉka. tumaruntu - tu ēval āyiṉa pōtum viṭṭicaiyāmaiyiṉ tumaruntu - nirai nēr nēr eṉka. (tu - uṇ)
‘ai ueo ivaikuṟiya maṟṟai
ēḻneṭ ṭeḻuttā nērap paṭumē’
‘ueo’ -viṭṭicaittut taṟcuṭṭiṉkaṇ iṭaiyilum iṟutiyilum taṉikkuṟilkaḷ nēracaiyāyiṉa.	(yā. ka. 7 urai)
taṉikkuṟil viṭṭicaikkum iṭaṅkaḷ -
taṉṉaittāṉē cuṭṭikkoḷḷumiṭam - ēvaṟporuḷ - kuṟippupporuḷ - eṉṟa immūviṭattum taṉikkuṟil viṭṭicaittu nēracaiyām.	(teṉ. yāp. 14)
e-ṭu : 
‘au aṟiyā aṟivil iṭaimakaṉē’ - taṟcuṭṭu
‘noalaiyil niṉāṭṭai nī’ - ‘nōva’ eṉṉum viṉai yeccam mutal kuṟuki ‘no’ eṉa niṉṟu ‘alaiyal’ eṉṉum ēvalporuṭṭāya viyaṅkōḷaik koṇṭu muṭintatu.
‘aā, iḻantāṉeṉ(ṟu) eṇṇap paṭum’ (nālaṭi. 9) - aā - avalattaik kāṭṭum kuṟippiṉkaṇ vanta iṭaiccol.		(yā. kā. 39 urai)
taṉiccīr -
nēricai veṇpā kaliveṇpā ivaṟṟil iraṇṭām aṭiyiṉ iṟutiyil etukai peṟṟuvarum cīr. iṉṉicai veṇpāvil mutal mūṉṟaṭikaḷum oruvikaṟpamāy ōraṭi etukai peṟṟuvara, mūṉṟām aṭiyiṉ iṟutic cīrum otta etukai peṟṟut taṉiccīrāka varutalum uṇṭu;mūṉṟu vikaṟpa iṉṉicai veṇpāvil, iraṇṭāmaṭiyiṉ iṟutic cīr taṉiccīrātalumām. orūu etukai peṟṟu varuvatu vēṟu; taṉiccīr vēṟu eṉappaṭum. taṉiccīr muṉṉaruḷḷa aṭiyiṉ mutaṟcīrōṭum otta etukai peṟuvatu; orūu etukai avvōraṭikkaṇṇēyē varuvatu.
taṉiccol (1) -
kalippāviṉuḷ uṟuppāka varum col.
akanilai ottāḻicaiyiṉ taṉiccol, tāḻicaikkup piṉṉum curitakattiṟku muṉṉum perumpālum ‘āṅku’ eṉṟa acaiccollāka varum; ‘eṉavāṅku’ eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa taṉiccoṟkaḷ poruḷ peṟṟum varum.
āṅku eṉṉum acaiccol payilum eṉavē, allātaṉa vākiya eṉavāṅku, eṉa ivaṉ, eṉaniṉ, ataṉāl, eṉanī, eṉanam, avaṉai, eṉṟuniṉ - eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa poruḷ peṟa varutal peṟṟām. ‘āṅku’ eṉpatu ‘āṅka’ eṉavum varum. ‘āṅku’ eṉpatu ēḻaṉurupāyp poruḷ uṇarttiṟ ṟēl, yāṇṭum poruḷ uṇarttutal vēṇṭum; aṅṅaṉam nillāmaiyiṉ acainilai āyiṟṟu.			(to. cey. 135 nac.)
taṉiccol tāḻicai muṉṉarum ciṟupāṉmai varum.			(131 iḷa.)
tāḻicaitōṟum taṉiccol varavum peṟum.	(128 iḷa.)
e-ṭu: kali. 118, 120, 130, 148
taṉiccol(2) -
iḵtu aṭimutaṟkaṇ poruḷ peṟa niṟpatu. itaṉaik kūṉ eṉavum vaḻaṅkuvar. nāṉku pāviṉum aṭimutaṟkaṇ cīr kūṉāy varappeṟum. eṉiṉum, vañciyaṭiyiṉ mutaṟkaṇ kūṉāy varuvatum, ukara īṟākiya nērīṟṟiyaṟcīr kūṉāy varuvatum ciṟappuṭaiyaṉa. vañciyaṭiyiṉ iṭaiyum iṟutiyum acai kūṉāy varuvataṉṟic cīr kūṉāy vārātu. āṇṭu ukara īṟākiya nērīṟṟiyaṟcīr arukik kūṉāy varavum peṟum. accīr, allāta pāviṉ aṭimutaṟkaṇ arukiyallatu kūṉāy vārātu. koccakakkaliyuḷ ōraṭi kūṉāy varumā yiṉum ciṟappillai. veṇpā āciriyam kali ivaṟṟiṉ aṭiyil iṭaiyum iṟutiyum kūṉ varappeṟātu.
e-ṭu:	vañci: ‘aṭi, atarcērtaliṉ akamcivantaṉa’
‘mā, eṟipatattāṉ iṭamkāṭṭa’ (puṟanā. 4)- mutaṟkaṇ acai kūṉāka vantatu.
‘vēntu, vēlvāṅkip piṭitturuttaliṉ’
teruvu, tērōṭat tēyntakaṉṟaṉa’ 
- ukara īṟṟu nērīṟṟu iyaṟcīr kūṉāka vantatu.
‘kalaṅkaḻāliṉ tuṟai kalakkuṟṟaṉa’- iṭaiyil acai kūṉāka vantatu.
‘māvaḻaṅkaliṉ mayakkuṟṟaṉa vaḻi’- kaṭaiyil acai kūṉāka vantatu.
āciriyam:
‘vaṭāatu, paṉipaṭu neṭuvarai vaṭakkum teṉāatu’- aṭimutaṟkaṇ ukara īṟākiya nērīṟṟiyaṟcīr kūṉ āyiṟṟu.
veṇpā:
‘utukkāṇ, curantāṉā vaṇkaic cuvaṇamāp pūtaṉ’- aṭimutaṟkaṇ cīr kūṉāka vantatu.
kali:
‘ulakiṉuḷ, peruntakaiyār peruntakaimai piṟaḻāvē piṟaḻiṉum’ aṭimutaṟkaṇ cīr kūṉāka vantatu.
‘ciṟukuṭi yīrē ciṟukuṭi yīrē’ (cilap 24 :  11) koccakak kaliyuḷ ōraṭiyē kūṉāka vantatu.	(yā. ka. 94)
taṉiccol : peyarkkāraṇam -
oru collāyp poruḷ nirampit taṉiyē niṟṟaliṉ taṉiccol eṉpatu kāraṇak kuṟiyām.	(yā. ka. 82)
taṉinilai aḷapeṭai -
cīrukku muṉṉum piṉṉum vēṟu eḻuttiṉṟi orōvō reḻuttē niṉṟu aḷapeṭuppatu.
e-ṭu: ‘ēe vaḻaṅkum cilaiyāy! iravaral
 māa vaḻaṅkum varai!’	(yā. ka. 41 urai)
taṉippātac ceyyuḷ -
ceyyuḷ mūvakaippaṭuvataṉkaṇ oṉṟu; (‘aṉṉaiyum pitāvum muṉṉaṟi teyvam’ eṉa) ōraṭiyāl naṭakkum pāṭal.		(vī. cō. 181 urai)
taṉiyacai - 	
ōracaiyākat taṉittu varuvatu. atu veṇpāviṉ iṟutikkaṇ nāḷ, malar eṉṉum vāypāṭṭāl varuvatum, vañciyaṭi mutal naṭu iṟutikkaṇ acai kūṉāka varuvatum pōlām.
tāa vaṇṇam -
poḻippum orūuvumāka etukai cīrkaḷil iṭaiyiṭṭu varat toṭuppatu
e-ṭu: ‘ulaka muvappa valaṉērpu tiritaru’ muruku.
‘uḷḷār kollō tōḻi muḷḷuṭai’	(aiṅ. mikaip. 2)
	(to. cey. 215 pērā.)
tāṇṭaka aṭi -
irupattāṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷāṉ iyaṉṟa aṭi			(yā. vi. pak. 476)
tāppicaip poruḷkōḷ -
ūñcalkayiṟu naṭuiṭattiṉiṉṟu irupuṟamum celvatu pōla, ceyyuḷ naṭuviluḷḷa collē muṉṉum piṉṉum poruḷ koḷḷac ceṉṟu amaiyum poruḷkōḷ itu.
e-ṭu: 	‘uṇṇāmai uḷḷa tuyirnilai ūṉuṇṇa
	 aṇṇāttal ceyyā taḷaṟu’	(kuṟaḷ 255)
itaṉkaṇ, ūṉ uṇṇāmai uḷḷatu uyirnilai, ūṉ uṇṇa aṇṇāttal ceyyātu aḷaṟu eṉa, naṭuniṉṟa ‘ūṉ’ eṉṟa collē irumaruṅkum ceṉṟu iyaintu poruḷ tantavāṟu.	(yā. ka. 95 urai)
tāya paṉuval -
aṟam poruḷ iṉpam mūṉṟaṟkum ilakkaṇam kūṟuvaṉa pōṉṟum, iṭaiyiṭaiyē aṉṟāyum tāvic celvatu. ilakkaṇam kūṟaliṉ, paṉuval; ilakkiyamātaliṉ ‘tāya’ eṉṟār. tāvutal - iṭaiyiṭutal.	(to. cey. 235 nac.)
tāvu vaṇṇam -
tāa vaṇṇam; tāvu vaṇṇamāvatu iṭaiyiṭṭu vanta etukaittāvatu. ‘tāa vaṇṇam’ kāṇka.	(vī. cō. 142)
tāḻampaṭutal -
ōcai tāḻntu varutal.	(yā.ka. 56 urai)
tāḻicai -
orupuṭaiyāṉ tattam pāviṉaṅkaḷōṭu ottalāṉum, aṭikaḷ ottut tāḻampaṭṭu icaittalāṉum, otta poruḷmēl perumpāṉmaiyum mūṉṟāyt tāḻntu icaittalāṉum tāḻicai eṉpatu kāraṇakkuṟi 			(yā. ka. 56 urai)
tāḻicai aḷavu -
itaṉ ciṟumai iraṇṭaṭi; perumai nāṉkaṭi, taravaḷaviṟkuc curuṅkiyum, ciṟupāṉmai camamāyum tāḻicai varum.			(yā. ka. 82 urai)
tāḻicai iṉṟit taravu peṟutal -
tāḻicaiyiṉṟit taravu māttiram varutal.
taravu ōcai peṟuvatu potuvāy niṉṟa koccaka orupōku eṉṉum ceyyuḷ. atu tāḻicaiyoṭu kūṭiya taravu ilak kaṇattil tirintu varum. taraviṉait tavira ēṉaiya uṟup pukkaḷ vilakkuṇṭu taravu taṉittuvarum. atu toṭarnilaic ceyyuḷāy varum tēvapāṇiyum, taraviṇaik koccakamāy varuvaṉavum ām. tāḻicai mutaliya iṉṟi varutaliṉ, taravu 4, 6, 8 eṉṟa aṭikaḷai uṭaittāy varal vēṇṭum eṉṟa varaiyaṟai yiṉṟu.
taravu taṉittu varutalēyaṉṟit taṉiccollum curitakamum peṟṟu varutalum, perumpāṉmai taraviṇaiyāy varutalum koḷḷappaṭum. avai taṉiccollum curitakamum peṟṟa taravukoccakam, taraviṇaik koccakam eṉak kūṟappaṭum. ivai tāḻampaṭṭa ōcaiyiṉṟi varutaliṉ tāḻicai eṉappaṭāmal taravu eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
e-ṭu: ‘pūṇāka eṉpaṇintāṉ pūtattāṉ vētattāṉ 
. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . 
. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . 
yāṉārvam ceyyum iṟai;

	‘eṉavāṅkup pāṭi iṟaiñcuvōrk kellām
. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . 
. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . 
niṉaiyā toḻiyaṟka neñcu”
itu taraviṇaik koccakam.
toṭarnilaic ceyyuḷiṉ mutaṟkaṇ niṉṟa tēvapāṇi taṉ poruḷoṭu tāṉ muṭiyum taṉit taravukoccakam.
e-ṭu : 	‘mūvā mutalā ulakammoru mūṉṟum ēttat
tāvāta iṉpam talaiyāyatu taṉṉiṉ eyti
ōvātu niṉṟa kuṇattoṇṇitic celvaṉ eṉpa
tēvāti tēvaṉ avaṉcēvaṭi cērtum aṉṟē’	(cīvaka. kaṭavuḷ.)
iṉi, tēvapāṇi allātu varum taravukoccakaṅkaḷ yāppiṉum poruḷiṉum vēṟṟumaiyuṭaiya taravukoc cakaṅkaḷām.	(to. cey. 149 nac.)
tāḻicaikkuṟaḷ -
itu kuṟaṭṭāḻicai eṉavumpaṭum. itu kuṟaḷveṇpāviṉ iṉaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. iraṇṭaṭikaḷāṉ amaiyum ik kuṟaṭṭāḻicai mūvakaippaṭum.
1. viḻumiya poruḷum oḻukiya ōcaiyum iṉṟi veṇ centuṟai pōlac camamāṉa īraṭikaḷāṉ amaivatu.
e-ṭu : ‘tiṭutim eṉaniṉ ṟumuḻā atirap
paṭitam payilkūt tarumārt taṉarē!’
2. iraṇṭaṭiyāy amaintu īṟṟaṭi cilacīrkaḷ kuṟaintu varuvatu.
e-ṭu : ‘naṇṇu vārviṉai naiya nāṭoṟum naṟṟa varkkara cāya ñāṉanaṟ
kaṇṇiṉāṉ aṭiyē aṭaivārkaḷ kaṟṟavarē’
itaṉ mutalaṭi eṇcīrttu; iraṇṭāmaṭi nāṟcīrttu.
3. taḷai vaḻuvi varum kuṟaḷveṇpā.
e-ṭu :	‘vaṇṭār iruṅkūntal varivaḷaikkai vāṇutalāḷ
paṇṭaiyaḷ allaḷ paṭi!’
mutalaṭi iraṇṭām mūṉṟām cīrkaḷiṭai kalittaḷai taṭṭu vantamaiyāṉ, kuṟaḷ veṇpā ākātu kuṟaṭṭāḻicai āyiṟṟu.
ivai iraṇṭaṭiyāyt tāḻntu icaittalāṉum, oḻukal ōcaiyiṉum ceppalōcaiyiṉum vaḻuvit tāḻnta ōcaiya vākalāṉum, viḻuppamillāta poruḷaic colluta lāṉum, tāḻicaik kuṟaḷ eṉavum kuṟaṭṭāḻicai eṉavum peyar peṟṟaṉa. kuṟaṭṭāḻicai ēḻutaḷaiyāṉum kūṟupaṭum. ivai ciṟappuṭaiya taḷai, ciṟappillāta taḷai eṉṟu pākupaṭuttappaṭavē, taḷai paṟṟik kuṟaṭṭāḻicai 14 vakaippaṭum.	(yā. ka. 64, 65 urai)
tāḻicai: peyarkkāraṇam -
otta oru poruḷ muṭiviṉāl otta tāḻcciyāl icaitta lāṉum, taraviṉ kuṟaintu icaittalāṉum ottāḻicai eṉavum tāḻicai eṉavumpaṭum.	(yā. ka. 82 urai)
tirikōṭṭa vēṇi -
iṭaiyīṭillāta nilattiṉai ‘orupōku’ eṉpa ātaliṉ, aḵtu oppiṉāṉ ākiya peyar. ‘orupōku’ eṉpataṉait ‘tirikōṭṭavēṇi’ eṉpatu pōlak koḷka (pērā. urai). tirinta kompu pōṉṟa vēṇiyai uṭaiyavaḷ eṉṟu, vēṇi vēṇiyai yuṭaiyavaḷaik kuṟittāṟpōla, pōkiya taṉaiyuṭaiyatai uṇarttumāṟu pōku eṉpatu vantatu. (vēṇi - kūntal)	(to. cey. 148 pērā.)
tiripāti -
mūṉṟaṭiyāyt tammuḷ aḷavottuvarum pattiyakkavi. itaṟku vīracōḻiya uraikārar eṭuttukkāṭṭu uraittilar. piṉvarum āciriyat tāḻicai (yā. ka. 75 urai) eṭuttuk kāṭṭātal takum.
e-ṭu :	‘vāṉuṟa nimirntaṉai; vaiyakam aḷantaṉai;
pāṉmati viṭuttaṉai; palluyir ōmpiṉai;
nīṉiṟa vaṇṇa! niṉ niraikaḻal toḻutaṉam.’ 	(vī. cō. 127)
tiripātip pōli -
mūṉṟaṭiyāyt tammuḷ aḷavu ottu vārāta pattiyakkavi. (uraiyuḷ icceyti kuṟikkappaṭāviṭiṉum, ‘kuṟaṭpōli’ pōṉṟavaṟṟatu urai nōkki ivvāṟu koḷḷappaṭṭatu. eṭuttukkāṭṭum mūṉṟaṭiyāy īṟṟaṭi iraṇṭum cīr kuṟaintuvanta ōroli veṇṭuṟai (yā. ka. 67 urai) kāṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. vīracōḻiya urai itaṟkum utāraṇam kāṭṭiṟṟilatu.
e-ṭu :	‘tāḷāḷa rallātār tāmpala rāyakkā leṉṉā meṉṉām
āḷiyaik kaṇṭañci āṉaitaṉ kōṭiraṇṭum
pīlipōṟ cāyntuviṭum piḷiṟṟi yāṅkē.’	(vī. cō. 127)
tilatak kalittuṟai -
kaṭṭaḷaik kalittuṟai vakai iraṇṭaṉuḷ oṉṟu; ēṉaiyatu kōvaik kalittuṟaiyām.
nēracai muṉvariṉ patiṉāṟu eḻuttāyum niraiyacai muṉvariṉ patiṉēḻu eḻuttāyum muṭiyum aṭikaḷ nālāyc cīr irupatiṉāl varuvatu tilatam eṉṉum peyarālē vaḻaṅkum.
(aṭitōṟum mutaṟcīr nāṉkum veṇṭaḷai piṟaḻātu, iṟutic cīroṉṟum viḷaṅkāyāka varappeṟutal vēṇṭum eṉṉum ilakkaṇam ‘kaṭṭaḷaik kalittuṟai’ eṉṉum talaippiṟ kāṇka.)
kōvaik kalittuṟai īṟṟaṭic cīrkaḷ vakaiyuḷiyāka amaita lum, tilatak kalittuṟai īṟṟaṭic cīrkaḷ vakaiyuḷi iṉṟi amaitalum tammuḷ vēṟupāṭu eṉṉum paḻaiyavurai.
‘nīraṇaṅ kōneñca mētaṉi yēyiṅku niṉṟavarē’	(tañcai. kō. 2)
‘tērmuntu pēralkul mātē aḵtu tilatamaṉṟē’	(vī. cō. 128)
(aḵtu : āytam alaku peṟṟatu.)
ivaiyiraṇṭum muṟaiyē kōvai tilatak kalittuṟaikaḷiṉ īṟṟaṭikaḷ; muṟaiyē vakaiyuḷiyuṭaimai iṉmaikaḷaik kāṇka.	(vī. cō. 128)
tilatap pōli -
tilatak kalittuṟaikkaṇ, orōvaṭiyil oṉṟātal iraṇ ṭātal eḻuttu mikkum kuṟaintum varuvatu tilatap pōli yām.
e-ṭu :	‘eṉavē taṉameṉ ṟirācēntira ciṅkaṉo ṭiṉṟaṇainta
kaṉavē yuṭaiyaṉ kaḷippukkaṇ ṭāṟkaṭa lēḻumamain
taṉavē yeṉavavaṉ ṟāṉutit taṉṟē toṭaṅkiyeṉṟu
naṉavē puṇartiru viṉkaḷik kētuko ṉallaṉavē’.
itaṉuḷ, mutalaṭikkaṇ ōreḻuttu mikku vantavāṟu. ‘irācēntra’ eṉṉātu ‘irācēntira’ eṉṟamaiyiṉ ōreḻuttu mikkuk kaṉiccīr viraviyavāṟu kāṇka. sa‘irā cēntra’ eṉak koḷḷiṉ viraviyalāy (-vaṭaveḻuttu viraviyatu) atu pōliyākāmaiyum uṇarappaṭum.]
	(vī. cō. 128)
tilatam -
tilatak kalittuṟaiyē ‘tilatam’ eṉa vaḻaṅkum. tilatam āmāṟu ‘tilatak kalittuṟai’ eṉṉum talaippiṟkāṇka.			(vī. cō. 128)
tukaḷ -
paṭai kuṭi kūḻ amaiccu naṭpu araṇ eṉṉumivaṟṟai aracaṉ pātukāvāmai (yākiya kuṟṟam).(tol. cey. 128 nac.)
tukaḷoṭum poruḷoṭum puṇartal -
aracaṉ taṉakkuriya araciyalil tappiya nilaiyaik kūṟum kūṟṟōṭum, avvaṟam poruḷ iṉpam payakkak kūṟum kūṟṟōṭum oru ceyyuḷ kūṭivarutal.	(to. cey. 128 nac.)
tuḷḷalōcai -
oḻuku naṭaittaṉṟi iṭaiyiṭai  uyarntu varutal; ‘kaṉṟu tuḷḷiṟṟu’ eṉṟāṟ pōlak koḷka.	(to. cey. 79 iḷa.)
muraṟkaip paṭumāṟṟāṉ tuḷḷac collum ōcai.
	(83 nac., pērā.)
vaḻakkiṉkaṇ perumpālum illātatāyc ceyyuṭkaṇ tuḷḷac collum ōcai kalippāviṟ kuriya tuḷḷal ōcaiyām.
tuḷḷalōcaiyiṉ vakaikaḷ -
	1. ēnticait tuḷḷal
kalittaḷaiyāṉ amaiyum ceyyuḷiṉ ōcai.
e-ṭu :	‘murukaviḻtā maraimalarmēl muṭiyimaiyōr puṭaivaravē
varuciṉaṉār tarumaṟainūl vaḻipiḻaiyā maṉamuṭaiyār
iruviṉaipōy viḻamuṟiyā etiriyakā tiyai eṟiyā
nirumalarāy aruviṉarāy nilavuvarcō tiyiṉiṭaiyē.’
	2. akaval tuḷḷal
kalittaḷaiyoṭu veṇcīr veṇṭaḷaiyum kalantamaiyum ceyyuḷiṉ ōcai.
e-ṭu :	‘celvappōrk katakkaṇṇaṉ ceyirtteṟinta ciṉavāḻi
mullaittār maṟamaṉṉar muṭittalaiyai murukkippōy
ellainīr viyaṉkoṇmū iṭainuḻaiyum matiyam pōl
mallalōṅ keḻilyāṉai marumampāynta toḷittatē’.
	3. pirinticait tuḷḷal
kalittaḷaiyōṭu ēṉaiya taḷaikaḷum kalantu amaiyum ōcaiyaiyuṭaiya kalippāviṉatu ōcai.
e-ṭu :	‘maṇikiḷar neṭumuṭi māyavaṉum tammuṉum pōṉ(ṟu)
aṇikiḷar neṭuṅkaṭalum kāṉalum tōṉṟumāl
nurainivan tavaiyaṉṉa noppaṟaiya ciṟaiyaṉṉam
irainayan tiṟaikūrum ēmañcāl tuṟaivakēḷ.’
ippāṭal niraiyoṉṟu āciriyattaḷaiyum iyaṟcīr veṇṭaḷaiyum, kalittaḷaiyum, veṇcīr veṇṭaḷaiyum, kalantamaiyiṉ pirinticait tuḷḷal ōcaittāyiṟṟu.
kalittāḻicaikaḷ īraṭiyiṉavāyavaḻip perumpāṉmaiyum pirinticait tuḷḷal ōcai peṟuvaṉavām.
e-ṭu :	‘toṉmaikkaṇ viṉaiceyyār tuppuraviṉ iraṅkuvār
paṉmaikkaṇ uḷḷantēr payamiṉṟik kaḻivārō’
eṉa varum.	(yā. ka. 78 urai)
tuṟai (1) -
oru puṭaiyāl tattam pāviṟkut tuṟai pōṉṟu neṟippāṭu uṭaittāyk kiṭattalāṉum, ellāt tuṟaimēlum iṉitu naṭattalāṉum, tuṟaiyum (ēṉaiya tāḻicai, viruttam pōlak) kāraṇappeyarām.	(yā. ka. 56 urai)
tuṟai (2) -
aivakai nilattiṟkum uriyar eṉappaṭṭa makkaḷum māvum paṟavaiyum varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭa varaiyaṟai kaṭantu, cāṉṟōr cuvaipaṭal karutic ceyyuḷ puṉaitalum uṇṭu. (avai) tattam tiṇaiyoṭu vārā āyiṉum, ettiṇai karutic ceyyappaṭṭaṉavō attiṇaip poruḷākak koḷḷattakkaṉa eṉṟu varaiyaṟuttuk kūṟum akappāṭṭu uṟupput tuṟai eṉappaṭum.
ñāḻalpūvaic cūṭiya neytalnilat talaiviyai ūriṭattuc cōlaiyākiya marutanilattuk kaṇṭāṉ pōlak kūṟik kuṟiñcip poruḷākiya puṇartal uripporuḷāṉ muṭitta kalippāṭal (56) kuṟiñcit tiṇaiyiṉpāṟpaṭutal ittuṟai eṉṟa uṟuppāṉ nikaḻum ceyalām.	(to. po. 521 pērā.)
uraippōrum kēṭpōrum iṉṟi, ceyyuḷ ceyyum pulavaṉē kavikkūṟṟāka uraippaṉavum tuṟai eṉṟa ceyyuḷ uṟuppiṉpāṟpaṭum eṉpa.	(na. a. 234; i. vi. 583)
tuṟai eṉṟa ivvuṟuppiṉṟiyum ceyyuḷ nikaḻum.
tuṟai (3) -
oru ceyal nikaḻvataṟkuk kāraṇam āyiṉavaṟṟaiyellām avaṟṟiṉ kāriyamākiya acceyaṟkaṇ ceṟittal. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka, paṭaiyiyaṅku aravam eṉpataṉkaṇ, nirai kōṭal kuṟitta maṉṉaṉ paṭait talaivarait taruka eṉṟa lum, avar varutalum, avar vantuḻi iṉṉatu ceyka eṉṟalum, avar vēntaṟku uraittalum, avar paṭaiyaik kūuy aṟivittalum, paṭaiccerukkum, ataṉaik kaṇṭōr kūṟalum, avar pakaippulak kēṭṭiṟku iraṅki varuntutalum, nāḷ kōṭalum, avar koṟṟavaikkup paravukkaṭaṉ pūṇṭalum eṉṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷ aṭaṅkum.			(i. vi. 603)
makkaḷum māvum ceṉṟu nīruṇṇum tuṟaipōlap pala vakaippaṭṭa poruḷkaḷum oruvakaippaṭṭu iyaṅkutalā kum mārkkam ‘tuṟai’ eṉappaṭṭatu.	(to. po. 56 nac.)
ovvoru tuṟaiyilum uṭpirivukaḷ pala iruttalum kūṭum.		(nac.)
oru ceyyuḷuḷ pala tuṟai oruṅkuvariṉum oru tuṟaiyiṉ pāṟpaṭutal koḷka.	(nac.)
tuṟai eṉṉum peyark kāraṇam -
parantuppaṭṭatoru nīrnilaiyuḷ evvaḻippukiṉum nīrai aṭaiyalām eṉiṉum, cila iṭaṅkaḷai vakuttukkoṇṭu avaṟṟait tuṟai eṉak kūṟi avvaḻippukku nīraikkoḷ vatu pōla, parantupaṭṭa puṟaoḻukalāṟṟiṉaic cila kūṟu paṭuttu avaṟṟiṉ vāyilākap pulappaṭac ceytalāṉ tuṟai eṉṟār. ceyyuḷuṟuppākak kūṟappaṭum tuṟai vēṟu, itu vēṟu.	(to. puṟat. 1 ca. pāla)
‘tūkkiyal vakaiyē āṅku’ -
tūkku eṉṉum ceyyuḷuṟuppu naṭakkum iṭavakai (-muṉ) collappaṭṭa āciriyam veṇpā kalivañci eṉṟa nāṉku pāvum ām.	(to. cey. 87 pērā.)
tūkku -
1.	centūkku, matalait tūkku, tuṇiput tūkku, kōyil tūkku, nivapput tūkku, kaḻāl tūkku, neṭuntūkku eṉa eḻuvakaippaṭum tāḷavakai.	(cilap. 14 : 150 urai)
2.	tāḷattil iraṇṭu māttiraikkālam.			(cilap. 3 - 16 urai)
tūkku eṉṉum ceyyuḷ uṟuppu -
niṟukkappaṭum poṉ veḷḷi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ kaṭṭikaḷaik kaḻañcu toṭi tulām eṉat tulākkōlāl tūkki aḷappatu pōla, aṭivaraiyaṟaiyuṭaiyavāyp paranta pākkaḷai aṭikaḷāl tuṇittut tūkki ōcai vēṟupāṭu uṇarttum ceyyuḷ uṟupput tūkku eṉappaṭṭatu. paṭavē, tūkku eṉpatu ōcai paṟṟip pākkaḷai iṉṉa pā eṉa varaiyaṟai ceyyum ceyyuḷuṟuppām.
tūkku eṉpatu niṟuttal, aḷattal, pāṭutal eṉṉum poruḷkaḷaik koḷḷum. pā eṉṟu parantupaṭṭuc cellum ōcaiyai ittuṇai aṭi eṉṟu vitantu kūṟupāṭu aṟiya utavuvatu tūkku.
irucīr mutal aintucīrkāṟum parantupaṭṭa akavalōcaiyai, ‘periyakaṭ peṟiṉē’ eṉac coṟcīraṭiyākavum, ‘yām pāṭat tāṉmakiḻn tuṇṇu maṉṉē’ eṉa aḷavaṭiyākavum, ‘narantam nāṟum taṉkaiyāl’ eṉac cintaṭiyākavum, tūkkut tuṇittavāṟu kāṇka. iṅṅaṉam tūkkiṉṟu eṉa,
‘narantam nāṟum taṉkaiyāṟ pulavu
nāṟum eṉtalai taivaru maṉṉē’	(puṟanā. 235)
eṉa iraṇṭu nāṟcīraṭikaḷākat tuṇippiṉ, akavalōcai piṟavātu.
‘uḷḷār kollō tōḻi
muḷḷuṭai alaṅkukulai īntiṉ cilampipoti ceṅkāy’			(aiṅ. mikaip. 2)
eṉa aṭi etukaiyāyc cintaṭiyum neṭilaṭiyumākak koḷḷa niṉṟataṉai, ittūkku eṉṉum uṟuppu, akavalōcaikku ēṟṟaṟpōla,
‘uḷḷār kollō tōḻi muḷḷuṭai
alaṅkukulai īntiṉ cilampipoti ceṅkāy’
eṉa aṟuttu iraṇṭu aḷavaṭi ākkiyatu.
tūkku illaiyēl āciriyattuḷ mayaṅki varum vañci yaṭikaḷaip pakuttuk kāṇṭal iyalātu.			(tol. cey. 87 nac., pērā.)
tūkku - iṟuti (iḷa.)
tūṅkal vaṇṇam -
tūṅkal ōcaiyuṭaittāy varum vaṇṇam; atu vañcipōl aṟṟuc cellutal.
e-ṭu :	‘yāṉūṭat tāṉuṇartta yāṉuṇarā viṭṭapiṉ
tāṉūṭa yāṉuṇarttat tāṉuṇarāṉ - tēṉūṉum’, 	(muttoḷ. 47)			(to. cey. 230 nac.)
tūṅka lōcai -
vaḻakkiṉkaṇ nikaḻātu, ceyyuṭkaṇ aṭiyiṟutiyil tūṅkātu (-ōcai aṟupaṭāmal) cīrtōṟum ōcai kuṟaintu varum tūṅkalōcai vañcippāviṟku uriyatām.			(to. cey. 84 nac.)
tūṅkal - cīrtōṟum ōcai aṟutal.	(80 iḷa.)
tūṅkicai -
ceyyuṭkuriya nālvakai ōcaiyuḷ oṉṟu.	(ṭu)
tūṅkicai akaval -
niraiyoṉṟu āciriyat taḷaiyāṉ varum akaval.
e-ṭu :	‘aṇiniḻa lacōkamarn taruṇeṟi naṭāttiya
maṇitika ḻaviroḷi varataṉaip		paṇipavar pavanaṉi paricaṟup pavarē!’	(yā. ka. 69 urai)
tūṅkicaic ceppal -
iyaṟcīr veṇṭaḷaiyāṉ varum veṇpāviṉ ōcai.
e-ṭu :	‘pāloṭu tēṉkalan taṟṟē paṇimoḻi
vāleyi ṟūṟiya nīr’. 	(ku. 1121)
ippāṭal muḻutum iyaṟcīr veṇṭaḷaiyē vantamaiyiṉ, itaṉkaṇ tūṅkicaic ceppalōcai vantatu.
iyaṟcīrē vantu veṇṭaḷai taṭpat tūṅkicaic ceppal piṟakkum eṉpa.	(yā. ka. 57 urai)
tūṅkicait tuḷḷal -
kalippāviṟku uriya ōcaivakai; kalittaḷaiyāl varum aṭikaḷil ivvōcai piṟakkum. ‘tuḷḷalōcaiyiṉ vakaikaḷ’ kāṇka.
tūṅkicai vaṇṇam irupatu -
kuṟil akaval tūṅkicaivaṇṇam, neṭil akaval tūṅkicai vaṇṇam, vali akaval tūṅkicaivaṇṇam, meli akaval tūṅkicai vaṇṇam, iṭai akaval tūṅkicaivaṇṇam - eṉa akaval tūṅkicaivaṇṇam aintu. (āciriyaṅkaḷum pāvaippāṭṭum pōlvaṉa ivvaṇṇattiṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭām.)
kuṟil oḻukal tūṅkicaivaṇṇam, neṭil oḻukal tūṅkicaivaṇṇam, vali oḻukal tūṅkicaivaṇṇam, meli oḻukal tūṅkicaivaṇṇam, iṭai oḻukal tūṅkicai vaṇṇam - eṉa oḻukal tūṅkicaivaṇṇam aintu. (vañciyum vañcippāviṉamum ivvaṇṇattiṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭām.)
kuṟil vallicait tūṅkicaivaṇṇam, neṭil vallicait tūṅkicaivaṇṇam, vali vallicait tūṅkicaivaṇṇam, meli vallicait tūṅkicaivaṇṇam, iṭai vallicait tūṅkicai vaṇṇam - eṉa valliṉat tūṅkicaivaṇṇam aintu.
e-ṭu :	‘pūvi ṉārpoḻiṟ piṇṭiyiṉkīḻp
poruvaru tirunakarp poṉṉeyiluḷ
māvi ṉārnalam nōkkiṉallār
palarpa ṇintuvan taṭivaṇaṅki’
eṉa iṉṉa vaṇṇattaṉa ellām vallicait tūṅkal 
vaṇṇattaṉa.
kuṟil mellicait tūṅkicaivaṇṇam, neṭil mel licait tūṅkicaivaṇṇam, vali mellicait tūṅkicai vaṇṇam, meli mellicait tūṅkicaivaṇṇam, iṭai mellicait tūṅkicai vaṇṇam eṉa mellicait tūṅkicaivaṇṇam aintu.
e-ṭu :	‘tēṉi ṉārmalarp piṇṭiyi ṉīḻal tēvarētta
vāṉi ṉārkuṭai  yāṉaṭi nāḷum vaṇaṅkutumē’
ivvāṟāka aivakaippaṭutaliṉ, tūṅkicaivaṇṇam irupatu āmāṟu.	(yā. vi. pak. 411, 412, 416 urai)
tēmā -
nēr nēr eṉa īracaiyāl amainta cīriṉaik kuṟikkum vāypāṭu.	(yā. kā. 7)
tēmāṅkaṉi -
nēr nēr nirai eṉa mūvacaiyāl amainta cīriṉaik kuṟikkum vāypāṭu.	(yā. kā. 7)
tēmāṅkāy -
nēr nēr nēr eṉa mūvacaiyāl amainta cīriṉaik kuṟikkum 
vāypāṭu.	(yā. kā. 7)
tēmāntaṇṇiḻal -
nēr nēr nēr nirai eṉa nālacaiyālamainta cīriṉaik kuṟikkum vāypāṭu.	(yā. kā. 8)
tēmāntaṇpū -
nēr nēr nēr nēr eṉa nālacaiyāl amainta cīriṉaik kuṟikkum vāypāṭu.	(yā. kā. 8)
tēmānaṟuniḻal -
nēr nēr nirai nirai eṉa nālacaiyāl amainta cīriṉaik kuṟikkum vāypāṭu.	(yā. kā. 8)
tēmānaṟumpū -
nēr nēr nirai nēr eṉa nālacaiyāl amainta cīriṉaik kuṟikkum vāypāṭu.	(yā. kā. 8)
tēvapāṇi -
tēvaraip pukaḻntu kūṟum tamiḻppāṭal vakai.
tēvapāṇi yāppum poruḷum vēṟupaṭutal -
‘veṇpalic cāntam muḻumeyyum meypūci.
uṇpalik kūrūr tirivatu mēliṭṭuk
kaṇpalik keṉṟu pukunta kapālimuṉ
eṇpalit tālivaḷ yātuvāy vāḷē.’
itu teyvattiṉmāṭṭu māṉiṭap peṇṭir nayanta pakka mākiya pāṭāṇtiṇaik kaikkiḷai yātaliṉ poruḷ vēṟupaṭṭatu.
‘kaṉṟu kuṇilā’ mutaliya cilappatikāra āycciyar kuravaip pāṭalkaḷ annilattiṟkuriya avarteyvattaip pāṭum pāṭalkaḷ; annilattiṟkuriya avarteyvattaip paravutaliṉ tēvapāṇiyē aṉṟi, puṟattiṇaik kaikkiḷaip pāṭalkaḷ alla. 	(to. cey.149 nac)
tēvapāṇiyiṉ iruvakai -
vaṇṇaka ottāḻicai eṉavum, oru pōku eṉavum tēvapāṇi iruvakaippaṭum. oru pōkukku ottāḻicai eṉap peyar kūṟappaṭātu eṉpatu; ataṉkaṇ tāḻicai peytu pāṭutalum kūṭātu. tolkāppiyaṉār orupōku tāḻicai yuṟuppup peṟum eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟilar. oru pōku ciṟupāṉmaiyē pāṭappeṟum. 	(to. cey. 139 nac.)
tēvarp parāvum ottāḻicai -
iḵtu ottāḻicaiyiṉ iruvakaikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. iḵtu akanilai ottāḻicai ākātu. vāḻttiyal nālvakaik kalikkaṇṇum varumēṉum, avai tēvapāṇi ākā. pāṭaṟkaṇ teyvavāḻttu paṭarkkaiyākiyavaḻi atu puṟanilai vāḻttēyām. pāṭṭuṭait talaivaṉaik kūṭṭik kūṟiṉum kūṭṭāviṭiṉum, tēvapāṇi muṉṉilaik kaṇṇa tēyām. teyvam tāṉē maṉitavaṭivil āvēcamuṟṟu, “niṉṉaip purappēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟum ulakavaḻakku pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟku ēlātu. kalippā pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkēyaṉṟic ciṟu pāṉmai kaṭavuḷaip parāvum poruḷāṉum varum. 			(to. cey. 138 nac.)
tokuttal vikāram -
aṟuvakaic ceyyuḷ vikāraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. ceyyuṭkaṇ ōcaiyum taḷaiyum karuti, virikkappaṭa vēṇṭiya urupu mutalāyiṉa tokukkappaṭutal.
e-ṭu : 	‘vēṇṭār vaṇakki viṟalmatil tāṉkōṭal
vēṇṭumām vēṇṭār makaṉ.’
‘vēṇṭārai vaṇakki’ eṉa uyartiṇaikkaṇ iraṇṭām vēṟṟumaiyurupu virintē varaṟpālatu, ceyyuḷamaippuk karuti ‘vēṇṭār vaṇakki’ eṉa urupu iṭaiyē tokuk kappaṭṭavāṟu. pakāppatamām oru moḻikkaṇ iṭaiyē ōreḻuttuk kuṉṟal iṭaikkuṟaiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum. 			(yā. vi. 394 pak)
tokainilai viri -
tokainilaiyum viriyumākak kūṟiya (āciriyam vañci veṇpā kali eṉṟa) nāṉku pā. 	(to. cey.120 nac.)
toṭai -
eḻuttu col poruḷ ivai mūṉṟaiyum porunti varicaippaṭa amainta iyalpiṉavāki, aṭiyōṭu aṭi poruntit toṭukkumpōtu amaivaṉa toṭaikaḷākum. (toṭaikku ellā eḻuttum koḷḷappaṭum.)			(yā. kā. 33 urai mēṟ.)
toṭai ōttu -
yāpparuṅkalattuḷ uṟuppiyaṟkaṇ āṟāvatākiya uṭpirivu; 21 nūṟpākkaḷaiyuṭaiyatu. mōṉai etukai muraṇ iyaipu aḷapeṭai eṉṉum toṭai aintaṉaiyum, iṇai poḻippu orūu kūḻai mēṟkatuvāy kīḻkkatuvāy muṟṟu aṭi eṉum eṭṭu vikaṟpaṅkaḷoṭum viḷakki, centoṭai iraṭṭait toṭai antātittoṭai eṉa mūṉṟaṉaiyum aṭuttu viḷakkic celvatu ivvōttu.
toṭaikaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai (1) -
nāṉku pākkaḷukkum 51 nilaṅkaḷ uḷa. avaṟṟiṉa virivu 625 aṭi. ovvōraṭiyum 22 toṭaipeṟum. toṭai iraṇṭaṭikaḷaik koṇṭu kāṇappaṭiṉum ōraṭiyiṉ kaṇṇēyē koḷḷappaṭutaliṉ 625 o 22 = 13750-il, ōraṭikkuriya 51 nilaṅkaḷaik kaḷaiya, 13699 ām. ivaṟṟai vikaṟpaṅkaḷoṭu kaṇakkiṭat toṭaiyeṇṇikkai varampiṉṟi ōṭum. 	(yā. ka. 49 urai).
toṭaikaḷiṉ eṇṇikkai (2) -
tolkāppiyaṉār toṭaikaḷiṉ eṇṇikkaiyai nērākak kūṟāmal, ‘aiyī rāyirat tāṟaiñ ñūṟṟoṭu, toṇṭutalai yiṭṭa pattuk kuṟai eḻunūṟ, ṟoṉpa teṉpa uṇarntici ṉōrē’ eṉṟār. eḻunūṟu oṉṟum - iḷampūraṇatu pāṭam.
viḷakkam varumāṟu :
aiyīrāyiram - pattāyiram - 10000
āṟaiññūṟu - mūvāyiram - 3000
toṇṭu talaiyiṭṭa pattukkuṟai eḻunūṟu oṉṟum - ṟoṉpatu - aṟunūṟṟut toṇṇūṟ ṟoṉpatu eṉpatu poruntum. 9 + (700-10)
pattukkuṟai eḻunūṟṟoṉpatu - 699; āka, 13699. toṇṭu talaiyiṭavē, 699+9 = 708; āka, 13708 eṉṟalum oṉṟu. (709-10) +9.
iḷampūraṇar 13699 eṉpar; pērāciriyar 13708 eṉpar. pattukkuṟai eḻunūṟṟoṉpatu - 699; toṇṭu talaiyiṭṭa eṉpatu oṉpatāl perukkiya eṉṟavāṟu; eṉavē, 699 o 9 = 6291 eṉak koṇṭu; 13000 + 6291 = 19291 eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭuvar, nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
ikkaṇakkīṭukaḷāl varaiyaṟukkappaṭum toṭaikaḷukku eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷ ikkālattil illai. iḷampūraṇar nērākak kaṇakkiṭavum, piṟa uraiyāciriyar iruvarum tampulamainalam tōṉṟa valintu palavāṟākak kaṇakkiṭuvar.
(1) iḷampūraṇar viḷakkam
	mōṉai
moḻi mutalākiya uyir 12. ippaṉṉiraṇṭu uyirkaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟaṟkum atu nīṅkalāṉa 11 eḻuttum kiḷaiyām. ākavē, ovvōruyirkkum kiḷaiyeḻuttup patiṉoṉṟu uḷa ātaliṉ avaṟṟai uṟaḻa, 12 o 11 = 132. katanapama eṉṉum meykaḷai uyir paṉṉiraṇṭōṭum uṟaḻa, 12 o 5 = 60. avaṟṟaik kiḷaiyeḻuttōṭu uṟaḻa, 60 o 11 = 660. cakarattiṉ mutaleḻuttu a ai oḷa nīṅkalākavuḷḷa oṉpataṉaik kiḷaiyeḻuttu eṭṭōṭum uṟaḻa, 9 o 8 = 72. vakarattiṉ mutaleḻuttu u ū o ō nīṅkalāka vuḷḷa eṭṭaṉaik kiḷai yeḻuttu ēḻōṭum uṟaḻa, 8 o 7 = 56. yakarattiṉ muta leḻuttu oṉṟē. ñakarattiṉ mutaleḻuttu a e o eṉṉu mūṉṟaṉaik kiḷaiyeḻuttu iraṇṭaṉōṭu uṟaḻa, 3 o 2 = 6.
ivvakaiyāṉ mutaleḻuttu 12 + 60 + 9 + 8 + 1 + 3 = 93. avaṟṟaik kiḷaiyeḻuttōṭu uṟaḻak kiḷaiyeḻuttu 132 + 660 + 72 + 56 + 6 = 926. mutaleḻuttu 93; kiḷaiyeḻuttu 926; āka, mōṉait toṭai 1019 ām.
	etukai
uyireḻuttu moḻiyiṭaiyē vārātu. uyirmey yeḻuttu 216 - il ṅakara varukkam paṉṉiraṇṭum oḻiya eñci yaṉa 204. ivaṟṟaik kiḷaiyeḻuttup paṉṉiraṇṭuṭaṉ uṟaḻa 204 o 12 = 2448. ivaṟṟōṭu mey 18; āytam 1; kuṟṟukaram 6. ivaṟṟaik kūṭṭa etukaiyiṉ kūṭutal 2473 ām.
	muraṇ
muraṇtoṭai col muraṇ, poruḷ muraṇ eṉa 2.
	iyaipu
moḻiyiṟutiyil vārāta uyirmeyyeḻuttukkaḷ varumāṟu : ṅakara varukka uyirmeykaḷ 12, u ū e ē o ō oḷa eṉṟa uyireḻuttukkaḷāl ēṟappaṭāta meykaḷ muṟaiyē 1, 1, 17, 1, 16, 1, 15 āka 52. ātaliṉ eñciya uyirmeyyeḻut tukkaḷ sa216 - (12 + 52)] 152, ñ, ṇ, n, m, ṉ, y, r, l, v, ḻ, ḷ eṉṟa meyyeḻuttukaḷ 11, kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu 6 - āka, ivvāṟṟāṉ iyaiputtoṭai 169 eṉka. saiḷampūraṇa ruraiyuḷ 182 eṉa (aṭikaḷāciriyar patippil) kāṇappaṭu kiṟatu.]
	aḷapeṭai
moḻi mutalākum uyiraḷapeṭai 7; ka ta na pa ma eṉum uyiraḷapeṭai 7 o 5 = 35; cakara aḷapeṭai 5; vakara aḷapeṭai 5; ñakara yakara aḷapeṭai 2; āka ivai 54. ivaṟṟuḷ uyiraḷapeṭai taṉinilaiyām 7 nīṅka, ēṉaiya 47 aiyum mutaṉilai iṭainilai iṟutinilai eṉa uṟaḻa, 47 o 3 = 141 uyiraḷapeṭaiyām. oṟṟukkaḷuḷ, valleḻuttu āṟum rakara ḻakaraṅkaḷum oḻinta ēṉaiya pattum āytamum aḷapeṭukka, oṟṟaḷapeṭai 11. ivvakaiyāl aḷapeṭait toṭai 7 + 141 + 11 = 159 ām.
ivvāṟṟāṉ mōṉai mutaliya aintoṭaikaḷiṉ kūṭutal 1019 + 2473 + 2 + 169 + 159 = 3822 ām. aṭikaḷāciriyar patippil 3835 eṉak kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atuvē koḷvōm.
	poḻipput toṭaiyil kiḷaiyeḻuttu vārātu
ātaliṉ mōṉaip poḻippu 93; etukaip poḻippu 229 (65 + 18 + 146 eṉak kāṇka). muraṇ poḻippu 2, iyaipu poḻippu 182, oṟṟaḷapeṭai poḻippāka vārāmaiyiṉ, uyiraḷapeṭaip poḻippu 148. ivaiyellām kūṭṭa, 93 + 229 + 2 + 182 + 148 = 654 ām.
	ōrūut toṭaiyum mēṟkaṇṭa vakaiyāṉ 654 ām.centoṭai
moḻimutalākiya eḻuttu 93. maṟṟai aṭiyilum avai ottu varuṅkāl avai mōṉaiyuḷ aṭaṅkutaliṉ, avaṟṟai oḻittu eñciya eḻuttu 92. ivaṟṟai uṟaḻa 93 o 92 = 8556 ām.
ākavē, ivvakaiyāṉ toṭai vikaṟpam 3835 + 654 + 654 + 8556 = 13669 ām. 	(to. cey. 97 iḷa.)
(2) pērāciriyar viḷakkam
pērāciriyarukkut toṭaikaḷ 32 + 1 = 33 ām. avaṟṟu eṇṇikkai varumāṟu : 
(1) mōṉaittoṭai - 566; (2) talaiyāku etukai - 566; (3) aṭi etukai 566; (4) eḻuttaṭi iyaipu 566; (5) collaṭi iyaipu - 566; ivaṟṟatu kūṭutal : 2830. (6) vaḻimōṉai - 549; (7) varukka mōṉai - 549; (8) uyiretukai - 549 (9) col muraṇ - 549 (10) poruḷ muraṇ 549; (11) collum poruḷum colloṭu muraṇ - 549; (12) collum poruḷum poruḷoṭu muraṇ - 549; (13) collum poruḷum colloṭum poruḷoṭum muraṇ - 549; ivaṟṟatu kūṭutal; 4392. (14) mūṉṟāmeḻuttoṉ ṟetukai - 479; (15) varukka etukai - 443; (16) neṭil mōṉai 270; (17) melliṉa etukai 270; (18) iṭaiyiṉa etukai - 270; (19) ācetukai - 270; (20) valliṉa etukai - 218; ivaṟṟatu kūṭutal : 2220 (21) uyiraḷapeṭai - 333; (22) oṟṟaḷapeṭai - 45; ivaṟṟatu kūṭutal : 378 (23,24) poḻippu, kiḷaippoḻippu - 551; (25,26) muṟṟu, kiḷai muṟṟu - 913; (27) orūu - 528; (28) kiḷai orūu - 530; ivaṟṟatu kūṭutal : 2522. (29) acai antāti - 325; (30) cīr antāti - 164; (31) eḻuttu viṭṭicai - 164; (32) kuṟippu viṭṭicai - 164; (33) centoṭai - 549; ivaṟṟatu kūṭutal : 1366.
ivvellāvaṟṟaiyum kūṭṭa, 13708 ām.			 (to. cey. 101 pērā.).
(3) nacciṉārkkiṉiyar viḷakkam
(1-5) mōṉai, etukai, muraṇ, iyaipu, aḷapeṭai eṉṉum aintu toṭaiyāṉa mūṉṟu pāviṟkum varum tokai - 2830 (6-15) aivakai muraṇ, varukka mōṉai, varukka etukai, vaḻi mōṉai, uyiretukai, centoṭai eṉṉum ivaṟṟāṉ varum tokai 5490.
(16) mūṉṟāmeḻuttoṉṟu etukai - 479. (17-20) melliṉa etukai, iṭaiyiṉa etukai, ācetukai, neṭilmōṉai eṉṉum ivaṟṟāl varum tokai - 1097.
(21) valliṉa etukai - 218
(22) uyiraḷapeṭai - 333
(23) oṟṟaḷapeṭai - 45
(24) acai antāti, cīrantāti - 272, 164.
(25) eḻuttu viṭṭicai, kuṟippu viṭṭicai - 164, 164.
āka, 25 toṭaiyāṉ peṟṟa tokai 11256 ām; ivai īraṭittoṭai.
iṉi ōraṭittoṭai ēḻu; avai varumāṟu :
(1) akavaṟpoḻippu - 578; (2) veṇpāp poḻippu - 441; (3) kalippoḻippu - 60; (4) muṟṟetukai - 153; (5) varukka muṟṟetukai - 395; (6) orūu etukai - 449; (7) varukka orūu etukai - 237; āka, ivaṟṟiṉ kūṭutal 2513; (iv veṇṇikkai ārāyaṟpālatu.) ivaṟṟuḷ nīkkappaṭuvaṉa - 79; eñciya, ōraṭittoṭaikaḷiṉ kūṭutal - 2434.
ivvāṟṟāl, 25 īraṭittoṭai 7 ōraṭittoṭai eṉṉum ivaṟṟiṉ kūṭutal 11256 + 2434 = 13690.
āciṭai etukai vēṟupāṭu, iraṇṭaṭi etukai, cen toṭai vēṟupāṭu, iraṭṭai, antāti vēṟupāṭu, iṇai, kūḻai, mēṟkatuvāy, kīḻkkatuvāy, muṟṟu, kaṭai yiṇai, kaṭaikkūḻai, iṭaippuṇar eṉṉumivaṟṟiṉ tokai - 5601.
ivvirutiṟamum kūṭṭa, 13690 + 5601 = 19291 ām (it toṭaik kaṇakkiṉai aṟivataṉāl payaṉ ciṟappāka yātum iṉṟu). 	(to. cey. 101 nac.)
toṭai 13,700 āmāṟu -
aṭivakaittoṭaikaḷ patiṉāṟum (5 aṭi vakait toṭaikaḷ cīrvakait toṭaikaḷ kāṇka) vaḻuvilavāy amainta 625 aṭikaḷil varum. ivaṟṟai uṟaḻa 10,000 toṭaikaḷ varumāṟu kāṇka.
cīrvakait toṭaikaḷ aimpataṉaip patiṉēḻ nilattavāy vaḻuviṉṟi varum aṭikaḷil payiṉṟu varutaṟkuriya 74 cīrka ḷōṭum uṟaḻa (50 o 74) 3,700 toṭaikaḷām.
patiṉēḻ nilattum payiṉṟu varum 74 cīrkaḷāvaṉa :
uriyacaiccīr - 2
iyaṟcīr - 10
āciriyavuriccīr - 6
veṇcīr - 4
vañciyuriccīr - 52 	(to. cey. 101 ca. pāla.)
toṭaikaḷuḷ ciṟantatu -
toṭaikaḷuḷ etukaittoṭaiyē ciṟantatām. ākavē, poḻippu mutaliya vikaṟpaṅkaḷai etukait toṭaikkē nūṟpāvil kūṟi, ēṉaiya toṭaikaḷukku aruttāpatti yāl koḷḷa vaittār tolkāppiyaṉār. 			(to. cey. 98 nac.)
‘toṭainilai vakaiyē āṅku’ -
cīrvakai aṭiyiṉ toṭaippakuti varaiyaṟaippaṭā eṉiṉum, avai ceyyuṭkaṇ niṟkum nilaimai muṟkūṟiya kaṭṭaḷai pōl niṉṟu toṭai koḷḷappeṟutal. to. cey. 103 nac.) āṅku - avviraṇṭu; varaiyaṟaiyuṭaiyaṉavum, illaṉavum (103 pērā.)
āṅku - mēṟcollappaṭṭa pākupāṭu; eḻuttāṉum collāṉum poruḷāṉum vēṟupaṭutal. 	(99 iḷa.)
toṭaippakuti eṇṇila ātal -
mōṉai etukai muraṇ iyaipu aḷapeṭai eṉpaṉa vaṟṟiṉkaṇ, iṇai kūḻai muṟṟu mēṟkatuvāy kīḻk katuvāy kaṭai kaṭaiyiṇai kaṭaikkūḻai iṭaippuṇar eṉa vēṟupaṭuttu uṟaḻavum, eḻuttantāti acaiyantāti cīrantāti aṭiyantāti eṉavum, uyirmōṉai uyiretukai neṭil mōṉai neṭiletukai varukkamōṉai varukkaetukai iṉamōṉai iṉaetukai ācetukai mūṉṟāmeḻut toṉṟetukai iṭaiyiṭṭetukai eṉavum ivvāṟu varuvaṉavaṟṟaiyum eḻuttu vēṟupāṭṭiṉāl uṟaḻavum, niralniṟaipporuḷkōḷ vakaiyāṉum uṟaḻavum, toṭaikaḷ varampilavāki viriyum. 	(tol. cey. 98 iḷa.).
625 vakaippaṭṭa aṭikaḷuḷ oṉṟai niṟutti, ataṉōṭu 624 aiyum kūṭṭiyum, ataṉōṭu cīrvakai aṭikaḷaiyum vikaṟpat toṭaikaḷaiyum kūṭṭiyum toṭukkat toṭaippakutikaḷ eṇṇilaṭaṅkā. 	(102 nac.)
toṭai pala viraviṉ peyar vaḻaṅkumāṟu -
oru ceyyuṭkaṇ toṭai pala viravi variṉum, mutalil vanta toṭaiyāṉ peyariṭappaṭum eṉpar yāpparuṅkala āciriyar; avinayaṉār, kākkaipāṭiṉiyār, ciṟukākkai pāṭiṉiyār, palkāyaṉār, nattattaṉār mutaliyōr karuttum ituvē. 
e-ṭu : 	‘tāmarai puraiyum kāmar cēvaṭi - poḻippetukai
pavaḷat taṉṉa mēṉit tikaḻoḷi’.
kuṉṟi yēykkum uṭukkai kuṉṟiṉ - orūuetukai
neñcupaka eṟinta añcuṭar neṭuvēl - poḻippetukai,
orūu mōṉai
cēvalaṅ koṭiyōṉ kāppa
ēma vaikal eytiṉṟāl ulakē’ - poḻippu mōṉai.
ippāṭaṟkaṇ ivvāṟu toṭaipala vantaṉa vāyiṉum, mutalil vanta toṭaiyāṉ peyarkoṭuttu, itaṉaip poḻippetukaic ceyyuḷ eṉṟu vaḻaṅkuka eṉpatu.
toṭaipaṟṟiya yāpparuṅkala virutti kuṟippiṭumāṟu -
nāṉku pākkaḷukkum 51 nilaṅkaḷ uḷa. avaṟṟiṉ virivu 625 aṭi. ovvōraṭiyum 22 toṭai peṟum. toṭai iraṇṭaṭikaḷaik koṇṭu kāṇappaṭiṉum orē aṭiyil koḷḷappaṭutaliṉ 625 o 22 = 13750 il ōraṭikkuriya nilaṅkaḷ 51 aik kaḷaiya toṭai 13699 ākum. (yā. ka. 49 urai)
toṭaimuraṇ -
muraṇtoṭai ‘cemmai pacumai’ - toṭai muraṇ 			(cīvaka. 2 nac.)
toṭaiyiṉ tokai -
mōṉai etukai muraṇ iyaipu aḷapeṭai centoṭai iraṭṭaittoṭai antātittoṭai eṉat toṭaiyiṉ tokai eṭṭām. 	(yā. ka. ciṟappup. urai)
toṭaivakai -
talaiyākumōṉai, iṭaiyāku mōṉai, kaṭaiyāku mōṉai - 3
talaiyākuetukai, iṭaiyāku etukai, kaṭaiyāku etukai - 3
colmuraṇ, poruḷ muraṇ, coṟporuḷ muraṇ, mōṉai muraṇ, etukai muraṇ, cemmuraṇ - 6.
mōṉaiiyaipu, etukaiiyaipu, muraṇiyaipu, aḷa peṭaiiyaipu, mayakku iyaipu, cevviyaipu - 6
mōṉai aḷapeṭai, etukai aḷapeṭai, muraṇ aḷapeṭai, iyaipu aḷapeṭai, mayakku aḷapeṭai, cevvaḷapeṭai - 6
iyaṟcentoṭai, maruṭcentoṭai - 2
oruporuḷiraṭṭai, iruporuḷiraṭṭai, pala poruḷi raṭṭai, iru muṟṟiraṭṭai - 4.
eḻuttantāti, acaiyantāti, cīrantāti, aṭiantāti, mayakkantāti, iṭaiyiṭṭantāti - 6
